0\leq\alpha\leq 1
. Let

\displaystyle\mathcal{M}_{h}^{3,\pm}(t,k,k_{1},k_{2},k_{3}):=

\displaystyle}{\displaystyle\frac{2}{k_{1}\Psi^{3}(k,k_{1},k_{2},k_{3})}e^{\pm it\Psi^{3}(k,k_{1},k_{2},k_{3})}

\displaystyle}{\displaystyle-\frac{2\sin^{2}\left(\frac{hk}{2}\right)\cos\left(\frac{hk}{4}\right)\cos\left(\frac{h(k_{2}+k_{3})}{4}\right)}{hk^{2}\sin\left(\frac{hk_{1}}{4}\right)\Phi_{h}^{3}(k,k_{1},k_{2},k_{3})}e^{\pm it\Phi_{h}^{3}(k,k_{1},k_{2},k_{3})}

over
\mathcal{A}(k)
defined as in (
5.7
). Then, we have

|\mathcal{M}_{h}^{3,\pm}(t,k,k_{1},k_{2},k_{3})|\lesssim\frac{h^{\alpha}(1+|t|)^{\alpha}(\max(|k|,|k_{1}|,|k_{2}|,|k_{3}|))^{2\alpha}}{|k_{1}|\max(|k_{1}|,|k_{2}|,|k_{3}|)}.

Proof.

Let

\mathcal{M}_{h,1}^{3,\pm}(t,k,k_{1},k_{2},k_{3}):=\frac{1}{k_{1}\Psi^{3}(k,k_{1},k_{2},k_{3})}\left(e^{\pm it\Psi^{3}(k,k_{1},k_{2},k_{3})}-e^{\pm it\Phi_{h}^{3}(k,k_{1},k_{2},k_{3})}\right)

and

\mathcal{M}_{h,2}^{3,\pm}(t,k,k_{1},k_{2},k_{3}):=\frac{1}{k_{1}\Psi^{3}(k,k_{1},k_{2},k_{3})}-\frac{\sin^{2}\left(\frac{hk}{2}\right)\cos\left(\frac{hk}{4}\right)\cos\left(\frac{h(k_{2}+k_{3})}{4}\right)}{hk^{2}\sin\left(\frac{hk_{1}}{4}\right)\Phi_{h}^{3}(k,k_{1},k_{2},k_{3})}.

Then,
\displaystyle\mathcal{M}_{h}^{3,\pm}(t,k,k_{1},k_{2},k_{3})=2\left(\mathcal{M}_{h,1}^{3,\pm}(t,k,k_{1},k_{2},k_{3})+\mathcal{M}_{h,2}^{3,\pm}(t,k,k_{1},k_{2},k_{3})e^{\pm it\Phi_{h}^{3}(k,k_{1},k_{2},k_{3})}\right)
, and thus it suffices to control
|\mathcal{M}_{h,1}^{3,\pm}(t,k,k_{1},k_{2},k_{3})|
and
|\mathcal{M}_{h,2}^{3,\pm}(t,k,k_{1},k_{2},k_{3})|
. Let
\displaystyle\mathcal{K}_{j\ell}=\frac{4}{h}\sin\left(\frac{h(k_{j}+k_{\ell})}{4}\right)
and
k_{j\ell}=k_{j}+k_{\ell}
,
j,\ell=1,2,3
. Then, it is known that
|\mathcal{K}_{j\ell}|\sim|k_{j\ell}|
,
j,\ell=1,2,3
. Similarly as the proof of (
6.8
), we have for
0\leq\alpha\leq 1
that

(6.11)

\left|e^{\pm it\Psi^{3}(k,k_{1},k_{2},k_{3})}-e^{\pm it\Phi_{h}^{3}(k,k_{1},k_{2},k_{3})}\right|\lesssim h^{\alpha}(1+|t|)^{\alpha}\left(|k_{12}||k_{13}||k_{23}|\max(|k_{12}|,|k_{13}|,|k_{23}|)\right)^{\alpha}.

Note that
\max(|k_{12}|,|k_{13}|,|k_{23}|)\lesssim\max(|k_{1}|,|k_{2}|,|k_{3}|)
and
|k_{12}k_{13}k_{23}|\gtrsim\max(|k_{1}|,|k_{2}|,|k_{3}|)
. Then, (
6.11
) guarantees

|\mathcal{M}_{h,1}^{3,\pm}(t,k,k_{1},k_{2},k_{3})|\lesssim\frac{h^{\alpha}(1+|t|)^{\alpha}(\max(|k_{1}|,|k_{2}|,|k_{3}|))^{\alpha}}{|k_{1}|(|k_{12}k_{13}k_{23}|)^{1-\alpha}}\lesssim\frac{h^{\alpha}(1+|t|)^{\alpha}(\max(|k_{1}|,|k_{2}|,|k_{3}|))^{2\alpha}}{|k_{1}|\max(|k_{1}|,|k_{2}|,|k_{3}|)}.

On the other hand, note by (
6.10
) that

(6.12)

\left|1-\cos\left(\frac{hk}{4}\right)\cos\left(\frac{hk_{23}}{4}\right)\right|\lesssim(h|k|+h|k_{23}|)^{\alpha}\lesssim h^{\alpha}(\max(|k|,|k_{2}|,|k_{3}|))^{\alpha},

for any
0\leq\alpha\leq 2
. Moreover, by (
2.7
), we have

\displaystyle\left|\frac{1}{k_{1}\Psi^{3}(k,k_{1},k_{2},k_{3})}-\frac{1}{\mathcal{K}_{1}\Phi_{h}^{3}(k,k_{1},k_{2},k_{3})}\right|\lesssim

\displaystyle}{\displaystyle\left|\frac{\mathcal{K}_{1}\mathcal{K}_{12}\mathcal{K}_{13}\mathcal{K}_{23}-k_{1}k_{12}k_{13}k_{23}}{k_{1}k_{12}k_{13}k_{23}\mathcal{K}_{1}\mathcal{K}_{12}\mathcal{K}_{13}\mathcal{K}_{23}}\right|

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle}{\displaystyle\left|\frac{(\mathcal{K}_{1}-k_{1})\mathcal{K}_{12}\mathcal{K}_{13}\mathcal{K}_{23}}{k_{1}k_{12}k_{13}k_{23}\mathcal{K}_{1}\mathcal{K}_{12}\mathcal{K}_{13}\mathcal{K}_{23}}\right|

\displaystyle}{\displaystyle+\left|\frac{k_{1}(\mathcal{K}_{12}-k_{12})\mathcal{K}_{13}\mathcal{K}_{23}}{k_{1}k_{12}k_{13}k_{23}\mathcal{K}_{1}\mathcal{K}_{12}\mathcal{K}_{13}\mathcal{K}_{23}}\right|

\displaystyle}{\displaystyle+\left|\frac{k_{1}k_{12}(\mathcal{K}_{13}-k_{13})\mathcal{K}_{23}}{k_{1}k_{12}k_{13}k_{23}\mathcal{K}_{1}\mathcal{K}_{12}\mathcal{K}_{13}\mathcal{K}_{23}}\right|

\displaystyle}{\displaystyle+\left|\frac{k_{1}k_{12}\mathcal{K}_{13}(\mathcal{K}_{23}-k_{23})}{k_{1}k_{12}k_{13}k_{23}\mathcal{K}_{1}\mathcal{K}_{12}\mathcal{K}_{13}\mathcal{K}_{23}}\right|

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle}{\displaystyle\frac{h^{\alpha}(|k_{1}|^{\alpha}+|k_{12}|^{\alpha}+|k_{13}|^{\alpha}+|k_{23}|^{\alpha})}{|k_{1}||k_{12}||k_{13}||k_{23}|}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle}{\displaystyle\frac{h^{\alpha}(\max(|k_{1}|,|k_{2}|,|k_{3}|))^{\alpha}}{|k_{1}|\max(|k_{1}|,|k_{2}|,|k_{3}|)},

for any
0\leq\alpha\leq 2
, and where
\mathcal{K}_{1}
is defined in the proof of Lemma
6.7
. Together with (
2.8
) and (
6.12
), we obtain

\displaystyle\mathcal{M}_{h,2}^{3,\pm}(t,k,k_{1},k_{2},k_{3})\lesssim

\displaystyle}{\displaystyle\left|\left(1-\frac{4\sin^{2}\left(\frac{hk}{2}\right)}{h^{2}k^{2}}\right)\frac{\cos\left(\frac{hk}{4}\right)\cos\left(\frac{hk_{23}}{4}\right)}{\mathcal{K}_{1}\Phi_{h}^{3}(k,k_{1},k_{2},k_{3})}\right|

\displaystyle}{\displaystyle+\left|\left(1-\cos\left(\frac{hk}{4}\right)\cos\left(\frac{hk_{23}}{4}\right)\right)\frac{1}{\mathcal{K}_{1}\Phi_{h}^{3}(k,k_{1},k_{2},k_{3})}\right|

\displaystyle}{\displaystyle+\left|\frac{1}{k_{1}\Psi^{3}(k,k_{1},k_{2},k_{3})}-\frac{1}{\mathcal{K}_{1}\Phi_{h}^{3}(k,k_{1},k_{2},k_{3})}\right|

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle}{\displaystyle\frac{h^{\alpha}(\max(|k|,|k_{1}|,|k_{2}|,|k_{3}|))^{\alpha}}{|k_{1}|\max(|k_{1}|,|k_{2}|,|k_{3}|)},

for any
0\leq\alpha\leq 1
. Since
(\max(|k|,|k_{1}|,|k_{2}|,|k_{3}|))^{\alpha}\lesssim(\max(|k|,|k_{1}|,|k_{2}|,|k_{3}|))^{2\alpha}
for
\alpha\geq 0
, we complete the proof.
∎

Lemma 6.9
.

Let
0<h\leq 1
and
0\leq\alpha\leq 1
. Let

\displaystyle\mathcal{M}_{h,1}^{4,\pm}(t,k,k_{1},k_{2},k_{3},k_{4}):=

\displaystyle}{\displaystyle\frac{2(k_{1}+k_{2})}{k_{3}\Psi^{3}(k,k_{1}+k_{2},k_{3},k_{4})}e^{\pm it\Psi^{4}(k,k_{1},k_{2},k_{3},k_{4})}

\displaystyle}{\displaystyle-\frac{4\sin^{2}\left(\frac{hk}{2}\right)}{h^{2}k^{2}}\frac{\sin\left(\frac{h(k_{1}+k_{2})}{2}\right)\cos\left(\frac{hk}{4}\right)\cos\left(\frac{h(k_{1}+k_{2}+k_{4})}{4}\right)}{\sin\left(\frac{hk_{3}}{4}\right)\Phi_{h}^{3}(k,k_{1}+k_{2},k_{3},k_{4})}e^{\pm it\Phi_{h}^{4}(k,k_{1},k_{2},k_{3},k_{4})}

and

\displaystyle\mathcal{M}_{h,2}^{4,\pm}(t,k,k_{1},k_{2},k_{3},k_{4}):=

\displaystyle}{\displaystyle\frac{1}{\Psi^{3}(k,k_{1}+k_{2},k_{3},k_{4})}e^{\pm it\Psi^{4}(k,k_{1},k_{2},k_{3},k_{4})}

\displaystyle}{\displaystyle-\frac{4\sin^{2}\left(\frac{hk}{2}\right)}{h^{2}k^{2}}\frac{\cos\left(\frac{hk}{4}\right)\cos\left(\frac{h(k_{1}+k_{2})}{4}\right)\cos\left(\frac{h(k_{3}+k_{4})}{4}\right)}{\Phi_{h}^{3}(k,k_{1}+k_{2},k_{3},k_{4})}e^{\pm it\Phi_{h}^{4}(k,k_{1},k_{2},k_{3},k_{4})}

over
\mathcal{B}(k)
defined as in (
5.8
). Let

\mathcal{M}_{h}^{4,\pm}(t,k,k_{1},k_{2},k_{3},k_{4})=\mathcal{M}_{h,1}^{4,\pm}(t,k,k_{1},k_{2},k_{3},k_{4})+\mathcal{M}_{h,2}^{4,\pm}(t,k,k_{1},k_{2},k_{3},k_{4}).

Then, we have

\displaystyle}{\displaystyle|\mathcal{M}_{h}^{4,\pm}(t,k,k_{1},k_{2},k_{3},k_{4})|

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle}{\displaystyle\frac{h^{\alpha}(1+|t|)^{\alpha}|k_{12}|}{|k_{3}k_{123}k_{124}k_{34}|}\left(\max(|k|,|k_{1}|,|k_{2}|,|k_{3}|,|k_{12}|,|k_{34}|,|k_{123}|,|k_{124}|)\right)^{\alpha}\left(|k_{123}k_{124}k_{34}|^{\alpha}+|k_{12}k_{1}k_{2}|^{\alpha}\right).

Proof.

Let
\mathcal{K}_{j}
,
\mathcal{K}_{j\ell}
, and
k_{j\ell}
be defined as in the proofs of Lemma
6.7
and
6.8
for
j,\ell=1,2,3,4
. Additionally, let
\displaystyle\mathcal{K}_{j\ell m}=\frac{4}{h}\sin\left(\frac{h(k_{j}+k_{\ell}+k_{m})}{4}\right)
and
k_{j\ell m}=k_{j}+k_{\ell}+k_{m}
,
j,\ell,m=1,2,3,4
. Similarly as the proof of (
6.8
), we have

(6.13)

\displaystyle}{\displaystyle\left|e^{\pm it\Psi^{4}(k,k_{1},k_{2},k_{3},k_{4})}-e^{\pm it\Phi_{h}^{4}(k,k_{1},k_{2},k_{3},k_{4})}\right|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle}{\displaystyle\left|e^{\pm it\Psi^{3}(k,k_{12},k_{3},k_{4})}-e^{\pm it\Phi_{h}^{3}(k,k_{12},k_{3},k_{4})}\right|+\left|e^{\pm it\Psi^{2}(k_{12},k_{1},k_{2})}-e^{\pm it\Phi_{h}^{2}(k_{12},k_{1},k_{2})}\right|

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle}{\displaystyle h^{\alpha}(1+|t|)^{\alpha}\left((|k_{123}k_{124}k_{34}|\max(|k_{123}|,|k_{124}|,|k_{34}|))^{\alpha}+(|k_{12}k_{1}k_{2}|\max(|k_{12}|,|k_{1}|,|k_{2}|))^{\alpha}\right),

for
0\leq\alpha\leq 1
. Note by (
6.10
) that

(6.14)

\displaystyle\left|1-\cos\left(\frac{hk_{12}}{4}\right)\cos\left(\frac{hk}{4}\right)\cos\left(\frac{hk_{124}}{4}\right)\right|\lesssim

\displaystyle}{\displaystyle(h|k_{12}|+h|k|+h|k_{124}|)^{\alpha}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle}{\displaystyle h^{\alpha}(\max(|k_{12}|,|k|,|k_{124}|))^{\alpha},

for any
0\leq\alpha\leq 2
. Moreover, by (
2.7
), we have

\displaystyle\left|\frac{k_{12}}{k_{3}k_{123}k_{124}k_{34}}-\frac{\mathcal{K}_{12}}{\mathcal{K}_{3}\mathcal{K}_{123}\mathcal{K}_{124}\mathcal{K}_{34}}\right|=

\displaystyle}{\displaystyle\left|\frac{k_{12}\mathcal{K}_{3}\mathcal{K}_{123}\mathcal{K}_{124}\mathcal{K}_{34}-\mathcal{K}_{12}k_{3}k_{123}k_{124}k_{34}}{k_{3}k_{123}k_{124}k_{34}\mathcal{K}_{3}\mathcal{K}_{123}\mathcal{K}_{124}\mathcal{K}_{34}}\right|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle}{\displaystyle\left|\frac{(k_{12}-\mathcal{K}_{12})\mathcal{K}_{3}\mathcal{K}_{123}\mathcal{K}_{124}\mathcal{K}_{34}}{k_{3}k_{123}k_{124}k_{34}\mathcal{K}_{3}\mathcal{K}_{123}\mathcal{K}_{124}\mathcal{K}_{34}}\right|

\displaystyle}{\displaystyle+\left|\frac{\mathcal{K}_{12}(\mathcal{K}_{3}-k_{3})\mathcal{K}_{123}\mathcal{K}_{124}\mathcal{K}_{34}}{k_{3}k_{123}k_{124}k_{34}\mathcal{K}_{3}\mathcal{K}_{123}\mathcal{K}_{124}\mathcal{K}_{34}}\right|

\displaystyle}{\displaystyle+\left|\frac{\mathcal{K}_{12}k_{3}(\mathcal{K}_{123}-k_{123})\mathcal{K}_{124}\mathcal{K}_{34}}{k_{3}k_{123}k_{124}k_{34}\mathcal{K}_{3}\mathcal{K}_{123}\mathcal{K}_{124}\mathcal{K}_{34}}\right|

\displaystyle}{\displaystyle+\left|\frac{\mathcal{K}_{12}k_{3}k_{123}(\mathcal{K}_{124}-k_{124})\mathcal{K}_{34}}{k_{3}k_{123}k_{124}k_{34}\mathcal{K}_{3}\mathcal{K}_{123}\mathcal{K}_{124}\mathcal{K}_{34}}\right|

\displaystyle}{\displaystyle+\left|\frac{\mathcal{K}_{12}k_{3}k_{123}k_{124}(\mathcal{K}_{34}-k_{34})}{k_{3}k_{123}k_{124}k_{34}\mathcal{K}_{3}\mathcal{K}_{123}\mathcal{K}_{124}\mathcal{K}_{34}}\right|

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle}{\displaystyle\frac{|k_{12}|h^{\alpha}(|k_{12}|^{\alpha}+|k_{3}|^{\alpha}+|k_{123}|^{\alpha}+|k_{124}|^{\alpha}+|k_{34}|^{\alpha})}{|k_{3}||k_{123}||k_{124}||k_{34}|}

for any
0\leq\alpha\leq 2
. Together with (
6.13
), (
2.8
) and (
6.14
), we obtain

\displaystyle}{\displaystyle\left|\mathcal{M}_{h,1}^{4,\pm}(t,k,k_{1},k_{2},k_{3},k_{4})\right|

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle}{\displaystyle\left|\frac{k_{12}}{k_{3}\Psi^{3}(k,k_{12},k_{3},k_{4})}\left(e^{\pm it\Psi^{4}(k,k_{1},k_{2},k_{3},k_{4})}-e^{\pm it\Phi_{h}^{4}(k,k_{1},k_{2},k_{3},k_{4})}\right)\right|

\displaystyle}{\displaystyle+\left|\left(1-\frac{4\sin^{2}\left(\frac{hk}{2}\right)}{h^{2}k^{2}}\right)\frac{\sin\left(\frac{hk_{12}}{2}\right)\cos\left(\frac{hk}{4}\right)\cos\left(\frac{hk_{124}}{4}\right)}{\sin\left(\frac{hk_{3}}{4}\right)\Phi_{h}^{3}(k,k_{12},k_{3},k_{4})}\right|

\displaystyle}{\displaystyle+\left|\left(1-\cos\left(\frac{hk_{12}}{4}\right)\cos\left(\frac{hk}{4}\right)\cos\left(\frac{hk_{124}}{4}\right)\right)\frac{\sin\left(\frac{hk_{12}}{4}\right)}{\sin\left(\frac{hk_{3}}{4}\right)\Phi_{h}^{3}(k,k_{12},k_{3},k_{4})}\right|

\displaystyle}{\displaystyle+\left|\frac{k_{12}}{k_{3}k_{123}k_{124}k_{34}}-\frac{\mathcal{K}_{12}}{\mathcal{K}_{3}\mathcal{K}_{123}\mathcal{K}_{124}\mathcal{K}_{34}}\right|

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle}{\displaystyle\frac{h^{\alpha}(1+|t|)^{\alpha}|k_{12}|}{|k_{3}k_{123}k_{124}k_{34}|}\left(\max(|k|,|k_{1}|,|k_{2}|,|k_{3}|,|k_{12}|,|k_{34}|,|k_{123}|,|k_{124}|)\right)^{\alpha}\left(|k_{123}k_{124}k_{34}|^{\alpha}+|k_{12}k_{1}k_{2}|^{\alpha}\right),

for any
0\leq\alpha\leq 1
. Analogously, we also have

\displaystyle}{\displaystyle\left|\mathcal{M}_{h,2}^{4,\pm}(t,k,k_{1},k_{2},k_{3},k_{4})\right|

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle}{\displaystyle\left|\frac{1}{\Psi^{3}(k,k_{12},k_{3},k_{4})}\left(e^{\pm it\Psi^{4}(k,k_{1},k_{2},k_{3},k_{4})}-e^{\pm it\Phi_{h}^{4}(k,k_{1},k_{2},k_{3},k_{4})}\right)\right|

\displaystyle}{\displaystyle+\left|\left(1-\frac{4\sin^{2}\left(\frac{hk}{2}\right)}{h^{2}k^{2}}\right)\frac{\cos\left(\frac{hk}{4}\right)\cos\left(\frac{hk_{12}}{4}\right)\cos\left(\frac{hk_{34}}{4}\right)}{\Phi_{h}^{3}(k,k_{12},k_{3},k_{4})}\right|

\displaystyle}{\displaystyle+\left|\left(1-\cos\left(\frac{hk}{4}\right)\cos\left(\frac{hk_{12}}{4}\right)\cos\left(\frac{hk_{34}}{4}\right)\right)\frac{1}{\Phi_{h}^{3}(k,k_{12},k_{3},k_{4})}\right|

\displaystyle}{\displaystyle+\left|\frac{1}{k_{123}k_{124}k_{34}}-\frac{1}{\mathcal{K}_{123}\mathcal{K}_{124}\mathcal{K}_{34}}\right|

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle}{\displaystyle\frac{h^{\alpha}(1+|t|)^{\alpha}|k_{12}|}{|k_{3}k_{123}k_{124}k_{34}|}\left(\max(|k|,|k_{1}|,|k_{2}|,|k_{3}|,|k_{12}|,|k_{34}|,|k_{123}|,|k_{124}|)\right)^{\alpha}\left(|k_{123}k_{124}k_{34}|^{\alpha}+|k_{12}k_{1}k_{2}|^{\alpha}\right),

for any
0\leq\alpha\leq 1
. Collecting all, we complete the proof.
∎

6.3.
Continuum limit to KdV: Proof of Proposition
3.9

We finally prove the convergence of decoupled FPU (
3.5
) to KdV (
3.6
). Let
T=T(\|u_{h,0}^{\pm}\|_{H^{s}(\mathbb{T}_{h})})>0
be the common existence time for the solution
v_{h}^{\pm}(t)
(resp.
w^{\pm}(t)
) to the decoupled FPU with initial data
u_{h,0}^{\pm}
constructed in Proposition
4.11
(resp. the KdV equation with initial data
\mathcal{L}_{h}u_{h,0}^{\pm}
constructed in Proposition
6.2
).

Proposition 6.10
.

Let
0\leq s\leq 1
. Let
f_{h}
and
g
be any
H^{s}(\mathbb{T}_{h})
and
H^{s}(\mathbb{T})
functions, respectively. Then,

\|P_{\geq\frac{\pi}{h}}\mathcal{L}_{h}f_{h}\|_{L^{2}(\mathbb{T})}\lesssim h^{s}\|f_{h}\|_{H^{s}(\mathbb{T}_{h})}\quad\mbox{and}\quad\|P_{\geq\frac{\pi}{h}}g\|_{L^{2}(\mathbb{T})}\lesssim h^{s}\|g\|_{H^{s}(\mathbb{T})},

where
\mathcal{L}_{h}
is the linear interpolation defined as in (
2.5
).

Proof.

It immediately follows from Bernstein’s inequality and the boundedness of the linear interpolation operator.
∎

Proposition 6.11

(Comparison between linear FPU and Airy flows)
.

Let
0\leq s\leq 5
and
|t|\leq 1
be fixed. Let
f_{h}
be any
H^{s}(\mathbb{T}_{h})
function. Then,

\left\|P_{\leq\frac{\pi}{h}}\left(S^{\pm}(t)\mathcal{L}_{h}f_{h}(t)-\mathcal{L}_{h}S_{h}^{\pm}(t)f_{h}(t)\right)\right\|_{L^{2}(\mathbb{T})}\lesssim h^{\frac{2s}{5}}\|f_{h}\|_{H^{s}(\mathbb{T}_{h})}.

Proof.

Note that for
0\leq s\leq 5
, by the mean-value theorem and the Taylor remainder theorem, we have

\displaystyle\left|e^{\pm it\frac{k^{3}}{24}}-e^{\pm it\frac{1}{h^{2}}\left(k-\frac{2}{h}\sin(\frac{hk}{2})\right)}\right|\lesssim

\displaystyle}{\displaystyle\min\left(1,|t|\left|\frac{k^{3}}{24}-\frac{1}{h^{2}}\left(k-\frac{2}{h}\sin\left(\frac{hk}{2}\right)\right)\right|\right)

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle}{\displaystyle\min\left(1,|t|h^{2}|k|^{5}\right)

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle}{\displaystyle|t|^{\frac{s}{5}}h^{\frac{2s}{5}}|k|^{s}.

Thus, we conclude for
|t|\leq 1
that

(6.15)

\displaystyle}{\displaystyle\left\|P_{\leq\frac{\pi}{h}}\left(S^{\pm}(t)\mathcal{L}_{h}f_{h}(t)-\mathcal{L}_{h}S_{h}^{\pm}(t)f_{h}(t)\right)\right\|_{L^{2}(\mathbb{T})}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle}{\displaystyle\left\|\left(e^{\pm it\frac{k^{3}}{24}}-e^{\pm it\frac{1}{h^{2}}\left(k-\frac{2}{h}\sin\left(\frac{hk}{2}\right)\right)}\right)\frac{4}{h^{2}k^{2}}\sin^{2}\left(\frac{hk}{2}\right)\widehat{f}_{h}(t,k)\right\|_{L^{2}(|k|\leq\frac{\pi}{h})}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle}{\displaystyle h^{\frac{2s}{5}}\|f_{h}(t)\|_{H^{s}(\mathbb{T}_{h})}.

∎

Proposition 6.12
.

Let
0\leq s\leq 1
and
0<h\leq 1
. For
1<M<\frac{\pi}{h}
, we have

(6.16)

\displaystyle}{\displaystyle\|P_{\leq M}(\mathcal{L}_{h}\mathcal{V}_{h}^{\pm}-\mathcal{W}^{\pm})(t)\|_{L^{2}(\mathbb{T})}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle}{\displaystyle h^{\frac{s}{2}}M^{\frac{3}{2}+\frac{s}{2}}\int_{0}^{t}(1+|t^{\prime}|)^{\frac{s}{2}}\|\mathcal{V}_{h}^{\pm}(t^{\prime})\|_{H^{s}(\mathbb{T}_{h})}^{2}\;dt^{\prime}

\displaystyle}{\displaystyle+M^{\frac{3}{2}}\int_{0}^{t}\|(\mathcal{L}_{h}\mathcal{V}_{h}^{\pm}-\mathcal{W}^{\pm})(t^{\prime})\|_{L^{2}(\mathbb{T})}\left(\|\mathcal{V}_{h}^{\pm}(t^{\prime})\|_{H^{s}(\mathbb{T})}+\|\mathcal{W}^{\pm}(t^{\prime})\|_{H^{s}(\mathbb{T})}\right)\;dt^{\prime},

for any
\mathcal{V}_{h}^{\pm}\in H^{s}(\mathbb{T}_{h})
and
\mathcal{W}^{\pm}\in H^{s}(\mathbb{T})
. The implicit constant does not depend on
h\in(0,1]
.

Proof.

From (
5.3
) and (
5.12
), we write for
|k|\leq M
that

\displaystyle(\mathcal{L}_{h}\mathcal{V}_{h}^{\pm}-\mathcal{W}^{\pm})(t,k)

\displaystyle=\int_{0}^{t}\mathcal{L}_{h}B_{1}^{\pm}(\mathcal{V}_{h}^{\pm},\mathcal{V}_{h}^{\pm})(t^{\prime},k)-D_{1}^{\pm}(\mathcal{W}^{\pm},\mathcal{W}^{\pm})(t^{\prime},k)\;dt^{\prime}

\displaystyle=\int_{0}^{t}\left(\mathcal{L}_{h}B_{1}^{\pm}(\mathcal{V}_{h}^{\pm},\mathcal{V}_{h}^{\pm})-D_{1}^{\pm}(\mathcal{L}_{h}\mathcal{V}_{h}^{\pm},\mathcal{L}_{h}\mathcal{V}_{h}^{\pm})\right)(t^{\prime},k)\;dt^{\prime}

\displaystyle}{\displaystyle+\int_{0}^{t}D_{1}^{\pm}(\mathcal{L}_{h}\mathcal{V}_{h}^{\pm}-\mathcal{W}^{\pm},\mathcal{L}_{h}\mathcal{V}_{h}^{\pm}+\mathcal{W}^{\pm})(t^{\prime},k)\;dt^{\prime}.

By (
6.2
) and the Minkowski inequality, we first have

\displaystyle}{\displaystyle\|P_{\leq M}D_{1}^{\pm}(\mathcal{L}_{h}\mathcal{V}_{h}^{\pm}-\mathcal{W}^{\pm},\mathcal{L}_{h}\mathcal{V}_{h}^{\pm}+\mathcal{W}^{\pm})(t^{\prime})\|_{L^{2}(\mathbb{T})}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle}{\displaystyle M^{\frac{3}{2}}\|(\mathcal{L}_{h}\mathcal{V}_{h}^{\pm}-\mathcal{W}^{\pm})(t^{\prime})\|_{L^{2}(\mathbb{T})}\left(\|\mathcal{L}_{h}\mathcal{V}_{h}^{\pm}(t^{\prime})\|_{L^{2}(\mathbb{T})}+\|\mathcal{W}^{\pm}(t^{\prime})\|_{L^{2}(\mathbb{T})}\right).

On the other hand, by (
2.6
) and the symmetry over
k_{1}
and
k_{2}
, a direct computation for
|k|\leq M
gives

\mathcal{F}\left(\mathcal{L}_{h}B_{1}^{\pm}(\mathcal{V}_{h}^{\pm},\mathcal{V}_{h}^{\pm})(t^{\prime},k)-D_{1}^{\pm}(\mathcal{L}_{h}\mathcal{V}_{h}^{\pm},\mathcal{L}_{h}\mathcal{V}_{h}^{\pm}))\right)(t^{\prime},k)=I_{1}(t^{\prime},k)+I_{2}(t^{\prime},k)+I_{3}(t^{\prime},k),

where

I_{1}(t^{\prime},k)=\mp i\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}k=k_{1}+k_{2}\\
|k_{1}|,|k_{2}|\leq\frac{\pi}{h}\\
k_{1}k_{2}\neq 0\end{subarray}}\mathcal{M}_{h,0}^{2,\pm}(t^{\prime},k,k_{1},k_{2})\widehat{\mathcal{L}_{h}\mathcal{V}_{h}^{\pm}}(t^{\prime},k_{1})\widehat{\mathcal{L}_{h}\mathcal{V}_{h}^{\pm}}(t^{\prime},k_{2}),

here
\mathcal{M}_{h,0}^{2,\pm}(t^{\prime},k,k_{1},k_{2})
is introduced in Lemma
6.6
,

I_{2}(t^{\prime},k)=\mp\frac{ik}{4}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}k=k_{1}+k_{2}\\
|k_{1}|,|k_{2}|\leq\frac{\pi}{h}\\
k_{1}k_{2}\neq 0\end{subarray}}e^{\pm it^{\prime}\Psi^{2}(k,k_{1},k_{2})}\left(\widehat{\mathcal{L}_{h}\mathcal{V}_{h}^{\pm}}-\widehat{\mathcal{V}_{h}^{\pm}}\right)(t^{\prime},k_{1})\left(\widehat{\mathcal{L}_{h}\mathcal{V}_{h}^{\pm}}+\widehat{\mathcal{V}_{h}^{\pm}}\right)(t^{\prime},k_{2}),

and

I_{3}(t^{\prime},k)=\mp\frac{ik}{4}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}k=k_{1}+k_{2}\\
|k_{1}|>\frac{\pi}{h}\;\text{or}\;|k_{2}|>\frac{\pi}{h}\\
k_{1}k_{2}\neq 0\end{subarray}}e^{\pm it^{\prime}\Psi^{2}(k,k_{1},k_{2})}\widehat{\mathcal{L}_{h}\mathcal{V}_{h}^{\pm}}(t^{\prime},k_{1})\widehat{\mathcal{L}_{h}\mathcal{V}_{h}^{\pm}}(t^{\prime},k_{2}).

By Lemma
6.6
and the Cauchy-Schwarz inequality, we have

\|P_{\leq M}I_{1}(t^{\prime})\|_{L^{2}(\mathbb{T})}\lesssim h^{\alpha}(1+|t^{\prime}|)^{\alpha}M^{\frac{3}{2}+\alpha}\|\mathcal{V}_{h}^{\pm}(t^{\prime})\|_{H^{2\alpha}(\mathbb{T}_{h})}\|\mathcal{V}_{h}^{\pm}(t^{\prime})\|_{H^{\alpha}(\mathbb{T}_{h})},

for any
0\leq\alpha\leq 1
. On the other hand, by the Cauchy-Schwarz inequality and Lemma
2.8
, we have

(6.17)

\displaystyle\|P_{\leq M}I_{2}(t^{\prime})\|_{L^{2}(\mathbb{T})}\lesssim

\displaystyle}{\displaystyle h^{\alpha}M^{\frac{3}{2}}\left(\sum_{|k|\leq\frac{\pi}{h}}\left|\widehat{\mathcal{L}_{h}\mathcal{V}_{h}^{\pm}}-\widehat{\mathcal{V}_{h}^{\pm}}\right|^{2}\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}\left(\sum_{|k|\leq\frac{\pi}{h}}\left|\widehat{\mathcal{L}_{h}\mathcal{V}_{h}^{\pm}}+\widehat{\mathcal{V}_{h}^{\pm}}\right|^{2}\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle}{\displaystyle h^{\alpha}M^{\frac{3}{2}}\|\mathcal{V}_{h}^{\pm}(t^{\prime})\|_{H^{\alpha}(\mathbb{T}_{h})}\left(\|\mathcal{L}_{h}\mathcal{V}_{h}^{\pm}(t^{\prime})\|_{L^{2}(\mathbb{T})}+\|\mathcal{V}_{h}^{\pm}(t^{\prime})\|_{L^{2}(\mathbb{T}_{h})}\right),

for any
0\leq\alpha\leq 2
. Moreover, by the Cauchy-Schwarz inequality, we have

(6.18)

\displaystyle\|P_{\leq M}I_{3}(t^{\prime})\|_{L^{2}(\mathbb{T})}\lesssim

\displaystyle}{\displaystyle M^{\frac{3}{2}}\|P_{>\frac{\pi}{h}}\mathcal{L}_{h}\mathcal{V}_{h}^{\pm}(t^{\prime})\|_{L^{2}(\mathbb{T})}\|\mathcal{L}_{h}\mathcal{V}_{h}^{\pm}(t^{\prime})\|_{L^{2}(\mathbb{T})}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle}{\displaystyle h^{\alpha}M^{\frac{3}{2}}\|\mathcal{L}_{h}\mathcal{V}_{h}^{\pm}(t^{\prime})\|_{H^{\alpha}(\mathbb{T})}\|\mathcal{L}_{h}\mathcal{V}_{h}^{\pm}(t^{\prime})\|_{L^{2}(\mathbb{T})},

for any
\alpha\geq 0
. Collecting all in addition to Lemma
2.6
, we obtain

\displaystyle}{\displaystyle\|P_{\leq M}(\mathcal{L}_{h}\mathcal{V}_{h}^{\pm}-\mathcal{W}^{\pm})(t)\|_{L^{2}(\mathbb{T})}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle}{\displaystyle h^{\alpha}M^{\frac{3}{2}+\alpha}\int_{0}^{t}(1+|t^{\prime}|)^{\alpha}\|\mathcal{V}_{h}^{\pm}(t^{\prime})\|_{H^{2\alpha}(\mathbb{T}_{h})}\|\mathcal{V}_{h}^{\pm}(t^{\prime})\|_{H^{\alpha}(\mathbb{T}_{h})}\;dt^{\prime}

\displaystyle}{\displaystyle+h^{\alpha}M^{\frac{3}{2}}\int_{0}^{t}\|\mathcal{V}_{h}^{\pm}(t^{\prime})\|_{H^{\alpha}(\mathbb{T}_{h})}\|\mathcal{V}_{h}^{\pm}(t^{\prime})\|_{L^{2}(\mathbb{T}_{h})}\;dt^{\prime}

\displaystyle}{\displaystyle+M^{\frac{3}{2}}\int_{0}^{t}\|(\mathcal{L}_{h}\mathcal{V}_{h}^{\pm}-\mathcal{W}^{\pm})(t^{\prime})\|_{L^{2}(\mathbb{T})}\left(\|\mathcal{V}_{h}^{\pm}(t^{\prime})\|_{L^{2}(\mathbb{T}_{h})}+\|\mathcal{W}^{\pm}(t^{\prime})\|_{L^{2}(\mathbb{T})}\right)\;dt^{\prime}.

Taking
2\alpha=s
, we complete the proof.
∎

Proposition 6.13
.

Let
0\leq s\leq 1
and
0<h\leq 1
. For
1<M<\frac{\pi}{h}
, we have

(6.19)

\displaystyle\left\|P_{M\leq\cdot\leq\frac{\pi}{h}}\left(\mathcal{L}_{h}\mathcal{V}^{\pm}_{h}-\mathcal{W}^{\pm}\right)(t)\right\|_{L^{2}(\mathbb{T})}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle}{\displaystyle\frac{1}{M^{\frac{1}{2}}}\sup_{t^{\prime}\in[0,t]}\left(\|(\mathcal{L}_{h}\mathcal{V}_{h}^{\pm}-\mathcal{W}^{\pm})(t^{\prime})\|_{L^{2}(\mathbb{T})}\big(1+\|\mathcal{V}_{h}^{\pm}(t^{\prime})\|_{H^{s}(\mathbb{T}_{h})}+\|\mathcal{W}^{\pm}(t^{\prime})\|_{H^{s}(\mathbb{T})}\big)^{2}\right)

\displaystyle}{\displaystyle+h^{\frac{s}{2}}(1+|t^{\prime}|)^{1+\frac{s}{2}}\sup_{t^{\prime}\in[0,t]}\left(\left(1+\|\mathcal{V}_{h}^{\pm}(t^{\prime})\|_{H^{s}(\mathbb{T}_{h})}\right)\|\mathcal{V}_{h}^{\pm}(t^{\prime})\|_{H^{s}(\mathbb{T}_{h})}^{2}\right)

\displaystyle}{\displaystyle+\int_{0}^{t}\|(\mathcal{L}_{h}\mathcal{V}_{h}^{\pm}-\mathcal{W}^{\pm})(t^{\prime})\|_{L^{2}(\mathbb{T})}\left(1+\|\mathcal{V}_{h}^{\pm}(t^{\prime})\|_{H^{s}(\mathbb{T}_{h})}+\|\mathcal{W}^{\pm}(t^{\prime})\|_{H^{s}(\mathbb{T})}\right)^{3}dt^{\prime}

for any
\mathcal{V}_{h}^{\pm}\in H^{s}(\mathbb{T}_{h})
and
\mathcal{W}^{\pm}\in H^{s}(\mathbb{T})
. The implicit constant does not depend on
h\in(0,1)
.

Proof.

From (

) and (

) in addition to (

), we write for

that

where

\widehat{J}_{1}(t,k)=-\frac{1}{4}\sum_{t^{\prime}=0,t}\mathcal{F}\left(\mathcal{L}_{h}\textup{B}_{2}^{\pm}(\mathcal{V}_{h}^{\pm},\mathcal{V}_{h}^{\pm})-\textup{D}_{2}^{\pm}(\mathcal{W}^{\pm},\mathcal{W}^{\pm})\right)(t^{\prime},k),

\widehat{J}_{2}(t,k)=\frac{1}{2}\sum_{t^{\prime}=0,t}\mathcal{F}\left(\mathcal{L}_{h}\textup{B}_{3}^{\pm}(\mathcal{V}_{h}^{\pm},\mathcal{V}_{h}^{\pm},\mathcal{V}_{h}^{\pm})-\textup{D}_{3}^{\pm}(\mathcal{W}^{\pm},\mathcal{W}^{\pm},\mathcal{W}^{\pm})\right)(t^{\prime},k),

\widehat{J}_{3}(t,k)=\int_{0}^{t}\left((\mathcal{F}_{h}(\mathcal{L}_{h}\mathcal{N}_{h,3}^{\pm}))_{\mathcal{R}}(\mathcal{V}_{h}^{\pm},\mathcal{V}_{h}^{\pm},\mathcal{V}_{h}^{\pm})-(\mathcal{F}\mathcal{N}_{3}^{\pm})_{\mathcal{R}}(\mathcal{W}^{\pm},\mathcal{W}^{\pm},\mathcal{W}^{\pm})\right)(t^{\prime},k)\;dt^{\prime},

and

\widehat{J}_{4}(t,k)=-\frac{1}{2}\int_{0}^{t}\mathcal{F}\left(\mathcal{L}_{h}\textup{B}_{4}^{\pm}(\mathcal{V}_{h}^{\pm},\mathcal{V}_{h}^{\pm},\mathcal{V}_{h}^{\pm},\mathcal{V}_{h}^{\pm})-\textup{D}_{4}^{\pm}(\mathcal{W}^{\pm},\mathcal{W}^{\pm},\mathcal{W}^{\pm},\mathcal{W}^{\pm})\right)(t^{\prime},k)\;dt^{\prime}.

J_{1}
estimate
. We write

\displaystyle\left(\mathcal{L}_{h}\textup{B}_{2}^{\pm}(\mathcal{V}_{h}^{\pm},\mathcal{V}_{h}^{\pm})-\textup{D}_{2}^{\pm}(\mathcal{W}^{\pm},\mathcal{W}^{\pm})\right)(t^{\prime})=

\displaystyle}{\displaystyle\left(\mathcal{L}_{h}\textup{B}_{2}^{\pm}(\mathcal{V}_{h}^{\pm},\mathcal{V}_{h}^{\pm})-\textup{D}_{2}^{\pm}(\mathcal{L}_{h}\mathcal{V}_{h}^{\pm},\mathcal{L}_{h}\mathcal{V}_{h}^{\pm})\right)(t^{\prime})

\displaystyle}{\displaystyle\widehat{J}_{11}+\widehat{J}_{12}+\widehat{J}_{13},

estimate

\displaystyle}{\displaystyle\|P_{M\leq\cdot\leq\frac{\pi}{h}}J_{21}(t^{\prime})\|_{L^{2}(\mathbb{T})}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle}{\displaystyle h^{\alpha}(1+|t^{\prime}|)^{\alpha}\Bigg(\sum_{M\leq|k|\leq\frac{\pi}{h}}\Bigg|\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}0<|k_{1}|,|k_{2}|\leq\frac{\pi}{h}\\
k_{1}+k_{2}\neq k\end{subarray}}\frac{|\widehat{\mathcal{V}_{h}^{\pm}}(t^{\prime},k_{1})||k_{2}|^{2\alpha}|\widehat{\mathcal{V}_{h}^{\pm}}(t^{\prime},k_{2})||\widehat{\mathcal{V}_{h}^{\pm}}(t^{\prime},k-k_{1}-k_{2})|}{|k_{1}||k-k_{1}-k_{2}|}\Bigg|^{2}\Bigg)^{\frac{1}{2}}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle}{\displaystyle h^{\alpha}(1+|t^{\prime}|)^{\alpha}\left(\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}0<|k_{1}|,|k_{2}|\leq\frac{\pi}{h}\\
k_{1}+k_{2}\neq k\end{subarray}}\frac{1}{|k_{1}|^{2}|k-k_{1}-k_{2}|^{2}}\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}

\displaystyle}{\displaystyle\times\left(\sum_{M\leq|k|\leq\frac{\pi}{h}}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}0<|k_{1}|,|k_{2}|\leq\frac{\pi}{h}\\
k_{1}+k_{2}\neq k\end{subarray}}|\widehat{\mathcal{V}_{h}^{\pm}}(t^{\prime},k_{1})|^{2}|k_{2}|^{4\alpha}|\widehat{\mathcal{V}_{h}^{\pm}}(t^{\prime},k_{2})|^{2}|\widehat{\mathcal{V}_{h}^{\pm}}(t^{\prime},k-k_{1}-k_{2})|^{2}\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle}{\displaystyle h^{\alpha}(1+|t^{\prime}|)^{\alpha}\|\mathcal{V}_{h}^{\pm}(t^{\prime})\|_{H^{2\alpha}(\mathbb{T}_{h})}\|\mathcal{V}_{h}^{\pm}(t^{\prime})\|_{L^{2}(\mathbb{T}_{h})}^{2},

for any
0\leq\alpha\leq 1
. For the other cases, we also have the same result. For
J_{22}
and
J_{23}
, similarly as (
6.17
) and (
6.18
), respectively, but using (
6.4
), we obtain

\displaystyle\|P_{M\leq\cdot\leq\frac{\pi}{h}}J_{22}(t^{\prime})\|_{L^{2}(\mathbb{T})}\lesssim

\displaystyle}{\displaystyle\frac{1}{M^{\frac{1}{2}}}\left(\sum_{|k|\leq\frac{\pi}{h}}\left|\widehat{\mathcal{L}_{h}\mathcal{V}_{h}^{\pm}}-\widehat{\mathcal{V}_{h}^{\pm}}\right|^{2}\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}

\displaystyle}{\displaystyle\times\left(\|\mathcal{L}_{h}\mathcal{V}_{h}^{\pm}(t^{\prime})\|_{L^{2}(\mathbb{T})}^{2}+\|\mathcal{L}_{h}\mathcal{V}_{h}^{\pm}(t^{\prime})\|_{L^{2}(\mathbb{T})}\|\mathcal{V}_{h}^{\pm}(t^{\prime})\|_{L^{2}(\mathbb{T}_{h})}+\|\mathcal{V}_{h}^{\pm}(t^{\prime})\|_{L^{2}(\mathbb{T}_{h})}^{2}\right)

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle}{\displaystyle\frac{h^{\alpha}}{M^{\frac{1}{2}}}\|\mathcal{V}_{h}^{\pm}(t^{\prime})\|_{H^{\alpha}(\mathbb{T}_{h})}\left(\|\mathcal{L}_{h}\mathcal{V}_{h}^{\pm}(t^{\prime})\|_{L^{2}(\mathbb{T})}+\|\mathcal{V}_{h}^{\pm}(t^{\prime})\|_{L^{2}(\mathbb{T}_{h})}\right)^{2},

for any
0\leq\alpha\leq 2
, and

\displaystyle\|P_{M\leq\cdot\leq\frac{\pi}{h}}J_{23}(t^{\prime})\|_{L^{2}(\mathbb{T})}\lesssim

\displaystyle}{\displaystyle\frac{1}{M^{\frac{1}{2}}}\|P_{>\frac{\pi}{h}}\mathcal{L}_{h}\mathcal{V}_{h}^{\pm}(t^{\prime})\|_{L^{2}(\mathbb{T})}\|\mathcal{L}_{h}\mathcal{V}_{h}^{\pm}(t^{\prime})\|_{L^{2}(\mathbb{T})}^{2}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle}{\displaystyle\frac{h^{\alpha}}{M^{\frac{1}{2}}}\|\mathcal{L}_{h}\mathcal{V}_{h}^{\pm}(t^{\prime})\|_{H^{\alpha}(\mathbb{T})}\|\mathcal{L}_{h}\mathcal{V}_{h}^{\pm}(t^{\prime})\|_{L^{2}(\mathbb{T})}^{2},

for any
\alpha\geq 0
. Collecting all and taking
2\alpha=s
in addition to Lemma
2.6
, we obtain for
t^{\prime}=0,t
that

(6.21)

\displaystyle\|P_{M\leq\cdot\leq\frac{\pi}{h}}J_{2}(t^{\prime})\|_{L^{2}(\mathbb{T})}\lesssim

\displaystyle}{\displaystyle\frac{1}{M^{\frac{1}{2}}}\|(\mathcal{L}_{h}\mathcal{V}_{h}^{\pm}-\mathcal{W}^{\pm})(t^{\prime})\|_{L^{2}(\mathbb{T})}\left(\|\mathcal{V}_{h}^{\pm}(t^{\prime})\|_{H^{s}(\mathbb{T}_{h})}+\|\mathcal{W}^{\pm}(t^{\prime})\|_{H^{s}(\mathbb{T})}\right)^{2}

\displaystyle}{\displaystyle+h^{\frac{s}{2}}(1+|t^{\prime}|)^{\frac{s}{2}}\|\mathcal{V}_{h}^{\pm}(t^{\prime})\|_{H^{s}(\mathbb{T}_{h})}^{3}+\frac{h^{\frac{s}{2}}}{M^{\frac{1}{2}}}\|\mathcal{V}_{h}^{\pm}(t^{\prime})\|_{H^{s}(\mathbb{T}_{h})}^{3}.

J_{3}
estimate
. We write

\left((\mathcal{L}_{h}\mathcal{N}_{h,3}^{\pm})_{\mathcal{R}}(\mathcal{V}_{h}^{\pm},\mathcal{V}_{h}^{\pm},\mathcal{V}_{h}^{\pm})-(\mathcal{N}_{3}^{\pm})_{\mathcal{R}}(\mathcal{W}^{\pm},\mathcal{W}^{\pm},\mathcal{W}^{\pm})\right)(t^{\prime})=J_{31}+J_{32}+J_{33}+J_{34},

where

\widehat{J}_{31}=\mp 2i\left(\frac{4\sin\left(\frac{hk}{2}\right)}{h^{2}k^{2}}\frac{\cos\left(\frac{hk}{2}\right)\cos\left(\frac{hk}{4}\right)}{\frac{4}{h}\sin\left(\frac{hk}{4}\right)}-\frac{1}{k}\right)|\widehat{\mathcal{V}_{h}^{\pm}}(t^{\prime},k)|^{2}\widehat{\mathcal{V}_{h}^{\pm}}(t^{\prime},k),

\widehat{J}_{32}=\mp\frac{2i}{k}\left(|\widehat{\mathcal{V}_{h}^{\pm}}|^{2}\widehat{\mathcal{V}_{h}^{\pm}}-|\widehat{\mathcal{L}_{h}\mathcal{V}_{h}^{\pm}}|^{2}\widehat{\mathcal{L}_{h}\mathcal{V}_{h}^{\pm}}\right)(t^{\prime},k),

\widehat{J}_{33}=\mp\frac{2i}{k}\left(|\widehat{\mathcal{L}_{h}\mathcal{V}_{h}^{\pm}}|^{2}\widehat{\mathcal{L}_{h}\mathcal{V}_{h}^{\pm}}-|\widehat{\mathcal{W}^{\pm}}|^{2}\widehat{\mathcal{W}^{\pm}}\right)(t^{\prime},k),

and

\widehat{J}_{34}=\pm\frac{2ih\sin^{3}\left(\frac{hk}{2}\right)}{h^{2}k^{2}}\widehat{\mathcal{V}_{h}^{\pm}}(t^{\prime},k)\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}k_{1}\in(\mathbb{T}_{h})^{*}\\
k_{1}\neq\pm k\end{subarray}}|\widehat{\mathcal{V}_{h}^{\pm}}(t^{\prime},k_{1})|^{2}.

One can easily see that

\displaystyle\|P_{M\leq\cdot\leq\frac{\pi}{h}}J_{32}(t^{\prime})\|_{L^{2}(\mathbb{T})}\lesssim

\displaystyle}{\displaystyle\frac{1}{M}\left(\sum_{|k|\leq\frac{\pi}{h}}\left|\widehat{\mathcal{L}_{h}\mathcal{V}_{h}^{\pm}}(k)-\widehat{\mathcal{V}_{h}^{\pm}}(k)\right|^{2}\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}\left(\|\mathcal{L}_{h}\mathcal{V}_{h}^{\pm}(t^{\prime})\|_{L^{2}(\mathbb{T})}+\|\mathcal{V}_{h}^{\pm}(t^{\prime})\|_{L^{2}(\mathbb{T}_{h})}\right)^{2}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle}{\displaystyle\frac{h^{\alpha}}{M}\|\mathcal{V}_{h}^{\pm}(t^{\prime})\|_{H^{\alpha}(\mathbb{T}_{h})}\left(\|\mathcal{L}_{h}\mathcal{V}_{h}^{\pm}(t^{\prime})\|_{L^{2}(\mathbb{T})}+\|\mathcal{V}_{h}^{\pm}(t^{\prime})\|_{L^{2}(\mathbb{T}_{h})}\right)^{2},

for any
0\leq\alpha\leq 2
due to Lemma
2.8
, and

\|P_{M\leq\cdot\leq\frac{\pi}{h}}J_{33}(t^{\prime})\|_{L^{2}(\mathbb{T})}\lesssim\frac{1}{M}\|(\mathcal{L}_{h}\mathcal{V}_{h}^{\pm}-\mathcal{W}^{\pm})(t^{\prime})\|_{L^{2}(\mathbb{T})}\left(\|\mathcal{L}_{h}\mathcal{V}_{h}^{\pm}(t^{\prime})\|_{L^{2}(\mathbb{T})}+\|\mathcal{W}^{\pm}(t^{\prime})\|_{L^{2}(\mathbb{T})}\right)^{2}.

Moreover, since

\left|\frac{2ih\sin^{3}\left(\frac{hk}{2}\right)}{h^{2}k^{2}}\right|\lesssim h\quad\mbox{and}\quad\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}k_{1}\in(\mathbb{T}_{h})^{*}\\
k_{1}\neq\pm k\end{subarray}}|\widehat{\mathcal{V}_{h}^{\pm}}(t^{\prime},k_{1})|^{2}\lesssim\|\mathcal{V}_{h}^{\pm}(t^{\prime})\|_{L^{2}(\mathbb{T}_{h})}^{2},

we have

\|P_{M\leq\cdot\leq\frac{\pi}{h}}J_{34}(t^{\prime})\|_{L^{2}(\mathbb{T})}\lesssim h\|\mathcal{V}_{h}^{\pm}(t^{\prime})\|_{L^{2}(\mathbb{T}_{h})}^{3}.

For the rest
J_{31}
, by (
2.8
), (
6.12
) and (
2.7
), we have

\displaystyle}{\displaystyle\|P_{M\leq\cdot\leq\frac{\pi}{h}}J_{31}(t^{\prime})\|_{L^{2}(\mathbb{T})}

\displaystyle}{\displaystyle\lesssim\left(\sum_{M\leq|k|\leq\frac{\pi}{h}}\left|\frac{4\sin\left(\frac{hk}{2}\right)}{h^{2}k^{2}}\frac{\cos\left(\frac{hk}{2}\right)\cos\left(\frac{hk}{4}\right)}{\frac{4}{h}\sin\left(\frac{hk}{4}\right)}-\frac{1}{k}\right|^{2}|\widehat{\mathcal{V}_{h}^{\pm}}(t^{\prime},k)|^{6}\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle}{\displaystyle\left(\sum_{M\leq|k|\leq\frac{\pi}{h}}\left(\frac{h^{\alpha}|k|^{\alpha}}{|k|}\right)^{2}|\widehat{\mathcal{V}_{h}^{\pm}}(t^{\prime},k)|^{6}\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle}{\displaystyle\frac{h^{\alpha}}{M}\|\mathcal{V}_{h}^{\pm}(t^{\prime})\|_{H^{\alpha}(\mathbb{T}_{h})}\|\mathcal{V}_{h}^{\pm}(t^{\prime})\|_{L^{2}(\mathbb{T}_{h})}^{2},

for any
0\leq\alpha\leq 2
. Collecting all and taking
\alpha=s
in addition to Lemma
2.6
, we obtain

(6.22)

\displaystyle}{\displaystyle\|P_{M\leq\cdot\leq\frac{\pi}{h}}J_{3}(t^{\prime})\|_{L^{2}(\mathbb{T})}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle}{\displaystyle\int_{0}^{t}\Bigg(\frac{1}{M}\|(\mathcal{V}_{h}^{\pm}-\mathcal{W}^{\pm})(t^{\prime})\|_{L^{2}(\mathbb{T})}\left(\|\mathcal{L}_{h}\mathcal{V}_{h}^{\pm}(t^{\prime})\|_{H^{s}(\mathbb{T})}+\|\mathcal{W}^{\pm}(t^{\prime})\|_{H^{s}(\mathbb{T})}\right)^{2}

\displaystyle}{\displaystyle+\frac{h^{s}}{M}\|\mathcal{V}_{h}^{\pm}(t^{\prime})\|_{H^{s}(\mathbb{T}_{h})}^{3}+h\|\mathcal{V}_{h}^{\pm}(t^{\prime})\|_{H^{s}(\mathbb{T}_{h})}^{3}\Bigg)\;dt^{\prime}.

J_{4}
estimate
. We write

\displaystyle}{\displaystyle\left(\mathcal{L}_{h}\textup{B}_{4}^{\pm}(\mathcal{V}_{h}^{\pm},\mathcal{V}_{h}^{\pm},\mathcal{V}_{h}^{\pm},\mathcal{V}_{h}^{\pm})-\textup{D}_{4}^{\pm}(\mathcal{W}^{\pm},\mathcal{W}^{\pm},\mathcal{W}^{\pm},\mathcal{W}^{\pm})\right)(t^{\prime})

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle}{\displaystyle\left(\mathcal{L}_{h}\textup{B}_{4}^{\pm}(\mathcal{V}_{h}^{\pm},\mathcal{V}_{h}^{\pm},\mathcal{V}_{h}^{\pm},\mathcal{V}_{h}^{\pm})-\textup{D}_{4}^{\pm}(\mathcal{L}_{h}\mathcal{V}_{h}^{\pm},\mathcal{L}_{h}\mathcal{V}_{h}^{\pm},\mathcal{L}_{h}\mathcal{V}_{h}^{\pm},\mathcal{L}_{h}\mathcal{V}_{h}^{\pm})\right)(t^{\prime})

\displaystyle}{\displaystyle+\textup{D}_{4}^{\pm}(\mathcal{L}_{h}\mathcal{V}_{h}^{\pm}-\mathcal{W}^{\pm},\mathcal{L}_{h}\mathcal{V}_{h}^{\pm},\mathcal{L}_{h}\mathcal{V}_{h}^{\pm},\mathcal{L}_{h}\mathcal{V}_{h}^{\pm})(t^{\prime})

\displaystyle}{\displaystyle+\textup{D}_{4}^{\pm}(\mathcal{W}^{\pm},\mathcal{L}_{h}\mathcal{V}_{h}^{\pm}-\mathcal{W}^{\pm},\mathcal{L}_{h}\mathcal{V}_{h}^{\pm},\mathcal{L}_{h}\mathcal{V}_{h}^{\pm})(t^{\prime})

\displaystyle}{\displaystyle+\textup{D}_{4}^{\pm}(\mathcal{W}^{\pm},\mathcal{W}^{\pm},\mathcal{L}_{h}\mathcal{V}_{h}^{\pm}-\mathcal{W}^{\pm},\mathcal{L}_{h}\mathcal{V}_{h}^{\pm})(t^{\prime})

\displaystyle}{\displaystyle+\textup{D}_{4}^{\pm}(\mathcal{W}^{\pm},\mathcal{W}^{\pm},\mathcal{W}^{\pm},\mathcal{L}_{h}\mathcal{V}_{h}^{\pm}-\mathcal{W}^{\pm})(t^{\prime}).

By (
6.5
), we immediately know

\displaystyle}{\displaystyle\|P_{M\leq\cdot\leq\frac{\pi}{h}}\textup{D}_{4}^{\pm}(\mathcal{L}_{h}\mathcal{V}_{h}^{\pm}-\mathcal{W}^{\pm},\mathcal{L}_{h}\mathcal{V}_{h}^{\pm},\mathcal{L}_{h}\mathcal{V}_{h}^{\pm},\mathcal{L}_{h}\mathcal{V}_{h}^{\pm})(t^{\prime})\|_{L^{2}(\mathbb{T})}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle}{\displaystyle\|(\mathcal{L}_{h}\mathcal{V}_{h}^{\pm}-\mathcal{W}^{\pm})(t^{\prime})\|_{L^{2}(\mathbb{T})}\|\mathcal{L}_{h}\mathcal{V}_{h}^{\pm}(t^{\prime})\|_{L^{2}(\mathbb{T})}^{3}.

Analogously, we have

\displaystyle}{\displaystyle\|P_{M\leq\cdot\leq\frac{\pi}{h}}\textup{D}_{4}^{\pm}(\mathcal{W}^{\pm},\mathcal{L}_{h}\mathcal{V}_{h}^{\pm}-\mathcal{W}^{\pm},\mathcal{L}_{h}\mathcal{V}_{h}^{\pm},\mathcal{L}_{h}\mathcal{V}_{h}^{\pm})(t^{\prime})\|_{L^{2}(\mathbb{T})}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle}{\displaystyle\|(\mathcal{L}_{h}\mathcal{V}_{h}^{\pm}-\mathcal{W}^{\pm})(t^{\prime})\|_{L^{2}(\mathbb{T})}\|\mathcal{W}^{\pm}(t^{\prime})\|_{L^{2}(\mathbb{T})}\|\mathcal{L}_{h}\mathcal{V}_{h}^{\pm}(t^{\prime})\|_{L^{2}(\mathbb{T})}^{2},

\displaystyle}{\displaystyle\|P_{M\leq\cdot\leq\frac{\pi}{h}}\textup{D}_{4}^{\pm}(\mathcal{W}^{\pm},\mathcal{W}^{\pm},\mathcal{L}_{h}\mathcal{V}_{h}^{\pm}-\mathcal{W}^{\pm},\mathcal{L}_{h}\mathcal{V}_{h}^{\pm})(t^{\prime})\|_{L^{2}(\mathbb{T})}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle}{\displaystyle\|(\mathcal{L}_{h}\mathcal{V}_{h}^{\pm}-\mathcal{W}^{\pm})(t^{\prime})\|_{L^{2}(\mathbb{T})}\|\mathcal{W}^{\pm}(t^{\prime})\|_{L^{2}(\mathbb{T})}^{2}\|\mathcal{L}_{h}\mathcal{V}_{h}^{\pm}(t^{\prime})\|_{L^{2}(\mathbb{T})},

and

\displaystyle\|P_{M\leq\cdot\leq\frac{\pi}{h}}\textup{D}_{4}^{\pm}(\mathcal{W}^{\pm},\mathcal{W}^{\pm},\mathcal{W}^{\pm},\mathcal{L}_{h}\mathcal{V}_{h}^{\pm}-\mathcal{W}^{\pm})(t^{\prime})\|_{L^{2}(\mathbb{T})}\lesssim\|(\mathcal{L}_{h}\mathcal{V}_{h}^{\pm}-\mathcal{W}^{\pm})(t^{\prime})\|_{L^{2}(\mathbb{T})}\|\mathcal{W}^{\pm}(t^{\prime})\|_{L^{2}(\mathbb{T})}^{3}.

For the rest, we write for
M<|k|\leq\frac{\pi}{h}
that

\mathcal{F}\left(\mathcal{L}_{h}\textup{B}_{4}^{\pm}(\mathcal{V}_{h}^{\pm},\mathcal{V}_{h}^{\pm},\mathcal{V}_{h}^{\pm},\mathcal{V}_{h}^{\pm})-\textup{D}_{4}^{\pm}(\mathcal{L}_{h}\mathcal{V}_{h}^{\pm},\mathcal{L}_{h}\mathcal{V}_{h}^{\pm},\mathcal{L}_{h}\mathcal{V}_{h}^{\pm},\mathcal{L}_{h}\mathcal{V}_{h}^{\pm})\right)(t^{\prime},k)=\widehat{J}_{41}+\widehat{J}_{42}+\widehat{J}_{43},

where

\widehat{J}_{41}=\pm\frac{i}{2}\sum_{(k_{1},k_{2},k_{3},k_{4})\in\mathcal{B}(k)}\mathcal{M}_{h}^{4,\pm}(t^{\prime},k,k_{1},k_{2},k_{3},k_{4})\widehat{\mathcal{V}_{h}^{\pm}}(t^{\prime},k_{1})\widehat{\mathcal{V}_{h}^{\pm}}(t^{\prime},k_{2})\widehat{\mathcal{V}_{h}^{\pm}}(t^{\prime},k_{3})\widehat{\mathcal{V}_{h}^{\pm}}(t^{\prime},k_{4}),

here
\mathcal{M}_{h}^{4,\pm}(t^{\prime},k,k_{1},k_{2},k_{3},k_{4})
and
\mathcal{B}(k)
are introduced in Lemma
6.9
and (
5.8
), respectively,

\displaystyle\widehat{J}_{42}=\pm\frac{i}{2}\sum_{(k_{1},k_{2},k_{3},k_{4})\in\mathcal{B}(k)}

\displaystyle}{\displaystyle\frac{2(k_{1}+k_{2})+k_{3}}{k_{3}\Psi^{3}(k,k_{1}+k_{2},k_{3},k_{4})}e^{\pm it^{\prime}\Psi^{4}(k,k_{1},k_{2},k_{3},k_{4})}

\displaystyle\times

\displaystyle}{\displaystyle\Big(\big(\widehat{\mathcal{L}_{h}\mathcal{V}_{h}^{\pm}}-\widehat{\mathcal{V}_{h}^{\pm}}\big)(t^{\prime},k_{1})\widehat{\mathcal{V}_{h}^{\pm}}(t^{\prime},k_{2})\widehat{\mathcal{V}_{h}^{\pm}}(t^{\prime},k_{3})\widehat{\mathcal{V}_{h}^{\pm}}(t^{\prime},k_{4})

\displaystyle}{\displaystyle+\widehat{\mathcal{L}_{h}\mathcal{V}_{h}^{\pm}}(t^{\prime},k_{1})\big(\widehat{\mathcal{L}_{h}\mathcal{V}_{h}^{\pm}}-\widehat{\mathcal{V}_{h}^{\pm}}\big)(t^{\prime},k_{2})\widehat{\mathcal{V}_{h}^{\pm}}(t^{\prime},k_{3})\widehat{\mathcal{V}_{h}^{\pm}}(t^{\prime},k_{4})

\displaystyle}{\displaystyle+\widehat{\mathcal{L}_{h}\mathcal{V}_{h}^{\pm}}(t^{\prime},k_{1})\widehat{\mathcal{L}_{h}\mathcal{V}_{h}^{\pm}}(t^{\prime},k_{2})\big(\widehat{\mathcal{L}_{h}\mathcal{V}_{h}^{\pm}}-\widehat{\mathcal{V}_{h}^{\pm}}\big)(t^{\prime},k_{3})\widehat{\mathcal{V}_{h}^{\pm}}(t^{\prime},k_{4})

\displaystyle}{\displaystyle+\widehat{\mathcal{L}_{h}\mathcal{V}_{h}^{\pm}}(t^{\prime},k_{1})\widehat{\mathcal{L}_{h}\mathcal{V}_{h}^{\pm}}(t^{\prime},k_{2})\widehat{\mathcal{L}_{h}\mathcal{V}_{h}^{\pm}}(t^{\prime},k_{3})\big(\widehat{\mathcal{L}_{h}\mathcal{V}_{h}^{\pm}}-\widehat{\mathcal{V}_{h}^{\pm}}\big)(t^{\prime},k_{4})\Big),

and

\displaystyle\widehat{J}_{43}=\pm\frac{i}{2}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}(k_{1},k_{2},k_{3},k_{4})\in\mathcal{B}(k)\\
\max(|k_{j}|:j=1,2,3,4)>\frac{\pi}{h}\end{subarray}}

\displaystyle}{\displaystyle\frac{2(k_{1}+k_{2})+k_{3}}{k_{3}\Psi^{3}(k,k_{1}+k_{2},k_{3},k_{4})}e^{\pm it^{\prime}\Psi^{4}(k,k_{1},k_{2},k_{3},k_{4})}

\displaystyle}{\displaystyle\times\widehat{\mathcal{L}_{h}\mathcal{V}_{h}^{\pm}}(t^{\prime},k_{1})\widehat{\mathcal{L}_{h}\mathcal{V}_{h}^{\pm}}(t^{\prime},k_{2})\widehat{\mathcal{L}_{h}\mathcal{V}_{h}^{\pm}}(t^{\prime},k_{3})\widehat{\mathcal{L}_{h}\mathcal{V}_{h}^{\pm}}(t^{\prime},k_{4}).

For
J_{41}
, let
k_{max}=\max(|k_{1}|,|k_{2}|,|k_{3}|,|k_{12}|,|k_{34}|,|k_{123}|,|k_{124}|)
. By Lemma
6.9
and
k_{12}=k_{123}-k_{3}
,
J_{41}
is divided by the following four terms:

(6.23)

\sum_{(k_{1},k_{2},k_{3},k_{4})\in\mathcal{B}(k)}\frac{k_{max}^{\alpha}|k_{123}k_{124}k_{34}|^{\alpha}}{|k_{123}k_{124}k_{34}|}\widehat{\mathcal{V}_{h}^{\pm}}(t^{\prime},k_{1})\widehat{\mathcal{V}_{h}^{\pm}}(t^{\prime},k_{2})\widehat{\mathcal{V}_{h}^{\pm}}(t^{\prime},k_{3})\widehat{\mathcal{V}_{h}^{\pm}}(t^{\prime},k_{4}),

(6.24)

\sum_{(k_{1},k_{2},k_{3},k_{4})\in\mathcal{B}(k)}\frac{k_{max}^{\alpha}|k_{123}k_{124}k_{34}|^{\alpha}}{|k_{3}k_{124}k_{34}|}\widehat{\mathcal{V}_{h}^{\pm}}(t^{\prime},k_{1})\widehat{\mathcal{V}_{h}^{\pm}}(t^{\prime},k_{2})\widehat{\mathcal{V}_{h}^{\pm}}(t^{\prime},k_{3})\widehat{\mathcal{V}_{h}^{\pm}}(t^{\prime},k_{4}),

(6.25)

\sum_{(k_{1},k_{2},k_{3},k_{4})\in\mathcal{B}(k)}\frac{k_{max}^{\alpha}|k_{12}k_{1}k_{2}|^{\alpha}}{|k_{123}k_{124}k_{34}|}\widehat{\mathcal{V}_{h}^{\pm}}(t^{\prime},k_{1})\widehat{\mathcal{V}_{h}^{\pm}}(t^{\prime},k_{2})\widehat{\mathcal{V}_{h}^{\pm}}(t^{\prime},k_{3})\widehat{\mathcal{V}_{h}^{\pm}}(t^{\prime},k_{4}),

and

(6.26)

\sum_{(k_{1},k_{2},k_{3},k_{4})\in\mathcal{B}(k)}\frac{k_{max}^{\alpha}|k_{12}k_{1}k_{2}|^{\alpha}}{|k_{3}k_{124}k_{34}|}\widehat{\mathcal{V}_{h}^{\pm}}(t^{\prime},k_{1})\widehat{\mathcal{V}_{h}^{\pm}}(t^{\prime},k_{2})\widehat{\mathcal{V}_{h}^{\pm}}(t^{\prime},k_{3})\widehat{\mathcal{V}_{h}^{\pm}}(t^{\prime},k_{4}),

whenever
0\leq\alpha\leq\frac{1}{2}
. Let
k_{123}^{*}=k-k_{123}
.

For (
6.23
), we first fix
\alpha<\frac{1}{2}
. Note that

\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}0<|k_{1}|,|k_{2}|,|k_{3}|<\frac{\pi}{h}\\
(k-k_{3})(k-k_{1}-k_{2})\neq 0\end{subarray}}\frac{|k_{2}|^{4\alpha}|\widehat{\mathcal{V}_{h}^{\pm}}(t^{\prime},k_{2})|^{2}}{|(k-k_{3})(k-k_{1}-k_{2})|^{2-2\alpha}}\lesssim\|\mathcal{V}_{h}^{\pm}(t^{\prime})\|_{H^{2\alpha}(\mathbb{T}_{h})}^{2}

and

\displaystyle}{\displaystyle\sum_{M\leq|k|\leq\frac{\pi}{h}}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}0<|k_{1}|,|k_{2}|,|k_{3}|<\frac{\pi}{h}\\
k_{123}\neq 0\end{subarray}}\frac{|k_{1}|^{4\alpha}|\widehat{\mathcal{V}_{h}^{\pm}}(t^{\prime},k_{1})|^{2}|k_{3}|^{4\alpha}|\widehat{\mathcal{V}_{h}^{\pm}}(t^{\prime},k_{3})|^{2}|k_{123}^{*}|^{4\alpha}|\widehat{\mathcal{V}_{h}^{\pm}}(t^{\prime},k_{123}^{*})|^{2}}{|k_{1}+k_{2}+k_{3}|^{2-2\alpha}}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle}{\displaystyle\|\mathcal{V}_{h}^{\pm}(t^{\prime})\|_{H^{2\alpha}(\mathbb{T}_{h})}^{6}.

Consequently,

(6.27)

\displaystyle}{\displaystyle\left(\sum_{M\leq|k|\leq\frac{\pi}{h}}|\eqref{eq:j_41-1}|^{2}\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle}{\displaystyle\Bigg(\sum_{M\leq|k|\leq\frac{\pi}{h}}\Bigg(\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}0<|k_{1}|,|k_{2}|,|k_{3}|<\frac{\pi}{h}\\
(k-k_{3})(k-k_{1}-k_{2})\neq 0\end{subarray}}\frac{|k_{2}|^{4\alpha}|\widehat{\mathcal{V}_{h}^{\pm}}(t^{\prime},k_{2})|^{2}}{|(k-k_{3})(k-k_{1}-k_{2})|^{2-2\alpha}}\Bigg)

\displaystyle}{\displaystyle\times\Bigg(\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}0<|k_{1}|,|k_{2}|,|k_{3}|<\frac{\pi}{h}\\
k_{123}\neq 0\end{subarray}}\frac{|k_{1}|^{4\alpha}|\widehat{\mathcal{V}_{h}^{\pm}}(t^{\prime},k_{1})|^{2}|k_{3}|^{4\alpha}|\widehat{\mathcal{V}_{h}^{\pm}}(t^{\prime},k_{3})|^{2}|k_{123}^{*}|^{4\alpha}|\widehat{\mathcal{V}_{h}^{\pm}}(t^{\prime},k_{123}^{*})|^{2}}{|k_{1}+k_{2}+k_{3}|^{2-2\alpha}}\Bigg)\Bigg)^{\frac{1}{2}}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle}{\displaystyle\|\mathcal{V}_{h}^{\pm}(t^{\prime})\|_{H^{2\alpha}(\mathbb{T}_{h})}^{4}.

Now we consider the case
\alpha=\frac{1}{2}
. It is not difficult to see that

\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}0<|k_{1}|,|k_{2}|,|k_{3}|<\frac{\pi}{h}\\
k_{123}^{*}k_{123}(k-k_{3})(k-k_{1}-k_{2})\neq 0\end{subarray}}\frac{k_{max}}{|k_{123}(k-k_{3})(k-k_{1}-k_{2})||k_{1}k_{2}k_{3}k_{123}^{*}|^{2}}\lesssim 1,

which yields

\displaystyle}{\displaystyle\left(\sum_{M\leq|k|\leq\frac{\pi}{h}}|\eqref{eq:j_41-1}|^{2}\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle}{\displaystyle\Bigg(\sum_{M\leq|k|\leq\frac{\pi}{h}}\Bigg(\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}0<|k_{1}|,|k_{2}|,|k_{3}|<\frac{\pi}{h}\\
k_{123}^{*}k_{123}(k-k_{3})(k-k_{1}-k_{2})\neq 0\end{subarray}}\frac{k_{max}}{|k_{123}(k-k_{3})(k-k_{1}-k_{2})||k_{1}k_{2}k_{3}k_{123}^{*}|^{2}}\Bigg)

\displaystyle}{\displaystyle\times\Bigg(\sum_{0<|k_{1}|,|k_{2}|,|k_{3}|<\frac{\pi}{h}}|k_{1}|^{2}|\widehat{\mathcal{V}_{h}^{\pm}}(t^{\prime},k_{1})|^{2}|k_{2}|^{2}|\widehat{\mathcal{V}_{h}^{\pm}}(t^{\prime},k_{2})|^{2}|k_{3}|^{2}|\widehat{\mathcal{V}_{h}^{\pm}}(t^{\prime},k_{3})|^{2}|k_{123}^{*}|^{2}|\widehat{\mathcal{V}_{h}^{\pm}}(t^{\prime},k_{123}^{*})|^{2}\Bigg)\Bigg)^{\frac{1}{2}}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle}{\displaystyle\|\mathcal{V}_{h}^{\pm}(t^{\prime})\|_{H^{1}(\mathbb{T}_{h})}^{4}.

For (
6.24
), we first fix
\alpha<\frac{1}{2}
. Note that

\sum_{0<|k_{1}|,|k_{2}|,|k_{3}|<\frac{\pi}{h}}\frac{|k_{1}|^{4\alpha}|\widehat{\mathcal{V}_{h}^{\pm}}(t^{\prime},k_{1})|^{2}|k_{123}^{*}|^{4\alpha}|\widehat{\mathcal{V}_{h}^{\pm}}(t^{\prime},k_{123}^{*})|^{2}}{|k_{3}|}\lesssim\|\mathcal{V}_{h}^{\pm}(t^{\prime})\|_{H^{2\alpha}(\mathbb{T}_{h})}^{4}

and

\sum_{M\leq|k|\leq\frac{\pi}{h}}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}0<|k_{1}|,|k_{2}|,|k_{3}|<\frac{\pi}{h}\\
(k-k_{3})(k-k_{1}-k_{2})\neq 0\end{subarray}}\frac{|k_{2}|^{4\alpha}|\widehat{\mathcal{V}_{h}^{\pm}}(t^{\prime},k_{2})|^{2}|k_{3}|^{4\alpha}|\widehat{\mathcal{V}_{h}^{\pm}}(t^{\prime},k_{3})|^{2}}{|(k-k_{3})(k-k_{1}-k_{2})|^{2-2\alpha}}\lesssim\|\mathcal{V}_{h}^{\pm}(t^{\prime})\|_{H^{2\alpha}(\mathbb{T}_{h})}^{4}.

Consequently,

\displaystyle}{\displaystyle\left(\sum_{M\leq|k|\leq\frac{\pi}{h}}|\eqref{eq:j_41-2}|^{2}\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle}{\displaystyle\Bigg(\sum_{M\leq|k|\leq\frac{\pi}{h}}\Bigg(\sum_{0<|k_{1}|,|k_{2}|,|k_{3}|<\frac{\pi}{h}}\frac{|k_{1}|^{4\alpha}|\widehat{\mathcal{V}_{h}^{\pm}}(t^{\prime},k_{1})|^{2}|k_{123}^{*}|^{4\alpha}|\widehat{\mathcal{V}_{h}^{\pm}}(t^{\prime},k_{123}^{*})|^{2}}{|k_{3}|}\Bigg)

\displaystyle}{\displaystyle\times\Bigg(\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}0<|k_{1}|,|k_{2}|,|k_{3}|<\frac{\pi}{h}\\
(k-k_{3})(k-k_{1}-k_{2})\neq 0\end{subarray}}\frac{|k_{2}|^{4\alpha}|\widehat{\mathcal{V}_{h}^{\pm}}(t^{\prime},k_{2})|^{2}|k_{3}|^{4\alpha}|\widehat{\mathcal{V}_{h}^{\pm}}(t^{\prime},k_{3})|^{2}}{|(k-k_{3})(k-k_{1}-k_{2})|^{2-2\alpha}}\Bigg)\Bigg)^{\frac{1}{2}}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle}{\displaystyle\|\mathcal{V}_{h}^{\pm}(t^{\prime})\|_{H^{2\alpha}(\mathbb{T}_{h})}^{4}.

On the other hand, it is not difficult to see that

\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}0<|k_{1}|,|k_{2}|,|k_{3}|<\frac{\pi}{h}\\
k_{123}^{*}(k-k_{3})(k-k_{1}-k_{2})\neq 0\end{subarray}}\frac{k_{max}^{2}}{|k_{3}|^{2}|(k-k_{3})(k-k_{1}-k_{2})||k_{1}k_{2}k_{3}k_{123}^{*}|^{2}}\lesssim 1,

which yields

\displaystyle}{\displaystyle\left(\sum_{M\leq|k|\leq\frac{\pi}{h}}|\eqref{eq:j_41-2}|^{2}\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle}{\displaystyle\Bigg(\sum_{M\leq|k|\leq\frac{\pi}{h}}\Bigg(\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}0<|k_{1}|,|k_{2}|,|k_{3}|<\frac{\pi}{h}\\
k_{123}^{*}(k-k_{3})(k-k_{1}-k_{2})\neq 0\end{subarray}}\frac{k_{max}^{2}}{|k_{3}|^{2}|(k-k_{3})(k-k_{1}-k_{2})||k_{1}k_{2}k_{3}k_{123}^{*}|^{2}}\Bigg)

\displaystyle}{\displaystyle\times\Bigg(\sum_{0<|k_{1}|,|k_{2}|,|k_{3}|<\frac{\pi}{h}}|k_{1}|^{2}|\widehat{\mathcal{V}_{h}^{\pm}}(t^{\prime},k_{1})|^{2}|k_{2}|^{2}|\widehat{\mathcal{V}_{h}^{\pm}}(t^{\prime},k_{2})|^{2}|k_{3}|^{2}|\widehat{\mathcal{V}_{h}^{\pm}}(t^{\prime},k_{3})|^{2}|k_{123}^{*}|^{2}|\widehat{\mathcal{V}_{h}^{\pm}}(t^{\prime},k_{123}^{*})|^{2}\Bigg)\Bigg)^{\frac{1}{2}}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle}{\displaystyle\|\mathcal{V}_{h}^{\pm}(t^{\prime})\|_{H^{1}(\mathbb{T}_{h})}^{4}.

This proves the case
\alpha=\frac{1}{2}
.

For (
6.25
), we first fix
\alpha<\frac{1}{2}
. Using
\displaystyle k_{12}=\frac{1}{2}(k_{123}+k_{124}-k_{34})
, one sees that

\frac{|k_{12}|^{\alpha}}{|k_{123}k_{124}k_{34}|}\lesssim\frac{1}{|k_{123}k_{124}k_{34}|^{1-\alpha}}.

An analogous argument to (
6.27
) guarantees

\left(\sum_{M\leq|k|\leq\frac{\pi}{h}}|\eqref{eq:j_41-3}|^{2}\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}\lesssim\|\mathcal{V}_{h}^{\pm}(t^{\prime})\|_{H^{2\alpha}(\mathbb{T}_{h})}^{4}.

Now we consider the case
\alpha=\frac{1}{2}
. Note that
|k_{12}|\leq\min\left(\max(|k_{1}|,|k_{2}|),\max(|k_{123}|,|k_{124}|,|k_{34}|)\right)
. Considering all cases of
k_{max}
, it follows that

\frac{k_{max}^{\frac{1}{2}}|k_{12}k_{1}k_{2}|^{\frac{1}{2}}}{|k_{123}k_{124}k_{34}||k_{1}k_{2}k_{3}k_{4}|}\lesssim\frac{1}{|k_{123}k_{124}k_{4}|}+\frac{1}{|k_{123}k_{34}k_{4}|}+\frac{1}{|k_{124}k_{34}k_{3}|}.

Then, (
6.25
) with each multiplier on the right-hand side is estimated as follows:

\displaystyle}{\displaystyle\left(\sum_{M\leq|k|\leq\frac{\pi}{h}}|\eqref{eq:j_41-3}|^{2}\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle}{\displaystyle\Bigg(\sum_{M\leq|k|\leq\frac{\pi}{h}}\Bigg(\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}0<|k_{1}|,|k_{2}|,|k_{3}|<\frac{\pi}{h}\\
k_{123}^{*}(k-k_{3})\neq 0\end{subarray}}\frac{|k_{1}|^{2}|\widehat{\mathcal{V}_{h}^{\pm}}(t^{\prime},k_{1})|^{2}}{|k_{123}^{*}(k-k_{3})|^{2}}\Bigg)

\displaystyle}{\displaystyle\times\Bigg(\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}0<|k_{1}|,|k_{2}|,|k_{3}|<\frac{\pi}{h}\\
k_{1}+k_{2}+k_{3}\neq 0\end{subarray}}\frac{|k_{2}|^{2}|\widehat{\mathcal{V}_{h}^{\pm}}(t^{\prime},k_{2})|^{2}|k_{3}|^{2}|\widehat{\mathcal{V}_{h}^{\pm}}(t^{\prime},k_{3})|^{2}|k_{123}^{*}|^{2}|\widehat{\mathcal{V}_{h}^{\pm}}(t^{\prime},k_{123}^{*})|^{2}}{|k_{1}+k_{2}+k_{3}|^{2}}\Bigg)\Bigg)^{\frac{1}{2}}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle}{\displaystyle\|\mathcal{V}_{h}^{\pm}(t^{\prime})\|_{H^{1}(\mathbb{T}_{h})}^{4},

\displaystyle}{\displaystyle\left(\sum_{M\leq|k|\leq\frac{\pi}{h}}|\eqref{eq:j_41-3}|^{2}\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle}{\displaystyle\Bigg(\sum_{M\leq|k|\leq\frac{\pi}{h}}\Bigg(\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}0<|k_{1}|,|k_{2}|,|k_{3}|<\frac{\pi}{h}\\
k_{123}^{*}(k-k_{1}-k_{2})\neq 0\end{subarray}}\frac{|k_{2}|^{2}|\widehat{\mathcal{V}_{h}^{\pm}}(t^{\prime},k_{2})|^{2}}{|k_{123}^{*}(k-k_{1}-k_{2})|^{2}}\Bigg)

\displaystyle}{\displaystyle\times\Bigg(\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}0<|k_{1}|,|k_{2}|,|k_{3}|<\frac{\pi}{h}\\
(k_{1}+k_{2}+k_{3})\neq 0\end{subarray}}\frac{|k_{1}|^{2}|\widehat{\mathcal{V}_{h}^{\pm}}(t^{\prime},k_{1})|^{2}|k_{3}|^{2}|\widehat{\mathcal{V}_{h}^{\pm}}(t^{\prime},k_{3})|^{2}|k_{123}^{*}|^{2}|\widehat{\mathcal{V}_{h}^{\pm}}(t^{\prime},k_{123}^{*})|^{2}}{|k_{1}+k_{2}+k_{3}|^{2}}\Bigg)\Bigg)^{\frac{1}{2}}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle}{\displaystyle\|\mathcal{V}_{h}^{\pm}(t^{\prime})\|_{H^{1}(\mathbb{T}_{h})}^{4},

and

(6.28)

\displaystyle}{\displaystyle\left(\sum_{M\leq|k|\leq\frac{\pi}{h}}|\eqref{eq:j_41-3}|^{2}\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle}{\displaystyle\Bigg(\sum_{M\leq|k|\leq\frac{\pi}{h}}\Bigg(\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}0<|k_{1}|,|k_{2}|,|k_{3}|<\frac{\pi}{h}\\
k-k_{1}-k_{2}\neq 0\end{subarray}}\frac{|k_{1}|^{2}|\widehat{\mathcal{V}_{h}^{\pm}}(t^{\prime},k_{1})|^{2}|k_{3}|^{2}|\widehat{\mathcal{V}_{h}^{\pm}}(t^{\prime},k_{3})|^{2}}{|k-k_{1}-k_{2}|^{2}}\Bigg)

\displaystyle}{\displaystyle\times\Bigg(\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}0<|k_{1}|,|k_{2}|,|k_{3}|<\frac{\pi}{h}\\
k-k_{3}\neq 0\end{subarray}}\frac{|k_{2}|^{2}|\widehat{\mathcal{V}_{h}^{\pm}}(t^{\prime},k_{2})|^{2}|k_{123}^{*}|^{2}|\widehat{\mathcal{V}_{h}^{\pm}}(t^{\prime},k_{123}^{*})|^{2}}{|(k-k_{3})k_{3}|^{2}}\Bigg)\Bigg)^{\frac{1}{2}}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle}{\displaystyle\|\mathcal{V}_{h}^{\pm}(t^{\prime})\|_{H^{1}(\mathbb{T}_{h})}^{4}.

For (
6.26
), we first fix
\alpha<\frac{1}{2}
. It follows from
\displaystyle k_{12}=k_{124}-k_{34}+k_{3}
that

\frac{|k_{12}|^{\alpha}}{|k_{3}k_{124}k_{34}|}\lesssim\frac{1}{|k_{3}k_{124}k_{34}|^{1-\alpha}}.

Similarly as (
6.28
), we obtain

\left(\sum_{M\leq|k|\leq\frac{\pi}{h}}|\eqref{eq:j_41-4}|^{2}\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}\lesssim\|\mathcal{V}_{h}^{\pm}(t^{\prime})\|_{H^{2\alpha}(\mathbb{T}_{h})}^{4}.

On the other hand, note that
|k_{12}|\leq\min\left(\max(|k_{1}|,|k_{2}|),\max(|k_{124}|,|k_{34}|,|k_{3}|)\right)
. By considering all possible cases of
k_{max}
, we find

\frac{k_{max}^{\frac{1}{2}}|k_{12}k_{1}k_{2}|^{\frac{1}{2}}}{|k_{3}k_{124}k_{34}||k_{1}k_{2}k_{3}k_{4}|}\lesssim\frac{1}{|k_{124}k_{2}|^{\frac{1}{2}}|k_{3}k_{4}|}+\frac{1}{|k_{3}k_{124}k_{34}|}+\frac{1}{|k_{3}k_{124}k_{4}|}.

Note that the bound
\displaystyle\frac{1}{|k_{124}k_{2}|^{\frac{1}{2}}|k_{3}k_{4}|}
appears only when
\max(|k_{1}|,|k_{2}|)\sim|k|\gg\min(|k_{1}|,|k_{2}|),|k_{3}|,|k_{4}|
. Let us define a set by

\widetilde{\mathcal{B}}(k)=\mathcal{B}(k)\cap\{(k_{1},k_{2},k_{3},k_{4})\in((\mathbb{T}_{h})^{*})^{4}:\max(|k_{1}|,|k_{2}|)\sim|k|\gg\min(|k_{1}|,|k_{2}|),|k_{3}|,|k_{4}|\},

and a function
g_{h,\sigma}
by
\widehat{g}_{h,\sigma}(t^{\prime},k)=|k|^{-\sigma}|k||\widehat{\mathcal{V}_{h}^{\pm}}(t^{\prime},k)|
for
\sigma\in\mathbb{R}
. Note that
|k_{1}|\sim|k_{124}|
on
\widetilde{\mathcal{B}}(k)
. Then, by Lemma
2.5
, we have

\displaystyle}{\displaystyle\left(\sum_{M\leq|k|\leq\frac{\pi}{h}}\left|\sum_{(k_{1},k_{2},k_{3},k_{4})\in\widetilde{\mathcal{B}}(k)}\frac{k_{max}^{\frac{1}{2}}|k_{12}k_{1}k_{2}|^{\frac{1}{2}}}{|k_{3}k_{124}k_{34}|}\widehat{\mathcal{V}_{h}^{\pm}}(t^{\prime},k_{1})\widehat{\mathcal{V}_{h}^{\pm}}(t^{\prime},k_{2})\widehat{\mathcal{V}_{h}^{\pm}}(t^{\prime},k_{3})\widehat{\mathcal{V}_{h}^{\pm}}(t^{\prime},k_{4})\right|^{2}\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle}{\displaystyle\|g_{h,\frac{1}{2}}g_{h,\frac{1}{2}}g_{h,1}g_{h,1}\|_{L^{2}(\mathbb{T}_{h})}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle}{\displaystyle\|g_{h,\frac{1}{2}}\|_{L^{4}(\mathbb{T}_{h})}\|g_{h,\frac{1}{2}}\|_{L^{4}(\mathbb{T}_{h})}\|g_{h,1}\|_{L^{\infty}(\mathbb{T}_{h})}\|g_{h,1}\|_{L^{\infty}(\mathbb{T}_{h})}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle}{\displaystyle\|g_{h,\frac{1}{2}}\|_{H^{\frac{1}{2}}(\mathbb{T}_{h})}\|g_{h,\frac{1}{2}}\|_{H^{\frac{1}{2}}(\mathbb{T}_{h})}\|g_{h,1}\|_{H^{1}(\mathbb{T}_{h})}\|g_{h,1}\|_{H^{1}(\mathbb{T}_{h})}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle}{\displaystyle\|\mathcal{V}_{h}^{\pm}(t^{\prime})\|_{H^{1}(\mathbb{T}_{h})}^{4}.

With the second bound, (
6.26
) is dealt with analogously to (
6.28
). Lastly, (
6.26
) with the third bound is estimated as follows:

\displaystyle}{\displaystyle\left(\sum_{M\leq|k|\leq\frac{\pi}{h}}\left|\sum_{(k_{1},k_{2},k_{3},k_{4})\in\widetilde{\mathcal{B}}(k)\setminus\mathcal{B}(k)}\frac{k_{max}^{\frac{1}{2}}|k_{12}k_{1}k_{2}|^{\frac{1}{2}}}{|k_{3}k_{124}k_{34}|}\widehat{\mathcal{V}_{h}^{\pm}}(t^{\prime},k_{1})\widehat{\mathcal{V}_{h}^{\pm}}(t^{\prime},k_{2})\widehat{\mathcal{V}_{h}^{\pm}}(t^{\prime},k_{3})\widehat{\mathcal{V}_{h}^{\pm}}(t^{\prime},k_{4})\right|^{2}\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle}{\displaystyle\Bigg(\sum_{M\leq|k|\leq\frac{\pi}{h}}\Bigg(\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}0<|k_{1}|,|k_{2}|,|k_{3}|<\frac{\pi}{h}\\
k_{123}^{*}\neq 0\end{subarray}}\frac{|k_{2}|^{2}|\widehat{\mathcal{V}_{h}^{\pm}}(t^{\prime},k_{2})|^{2}}{|k_{123}^{*}k_{3}|^{2}}\Bigg)

\displaystyle}{\displaystyle\times\Bigg(\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}0<|k_{1}|,|k_{2}|,|k_{3}|<\frac{\pi}{h}\\
k-k_{3}\neq 0\end{subarray}}\frac{|k_{1}|^{2}|\widehat{\mathcal{V}_{h}^{\pm}}(t^{\prime},k_{1})|^{2}|k_{3}|^{2}|\widehat{\mathcal{V}_{h}^{\pm}}(t^{\prime},k_{3})|^{2}|k_{123}^{*}|^{2}|\widehat{\mathcal{V}_{h}^{\pm}}(t^{\prime},k_{123}^{*})|^{2}}{|k-k_{3}|^{2}}\Bigg)\Bigg)^{\frac{1}{2}}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle}{\displaystyle\|\mathcal{V}_{h}^{\pm}(t^{\prime})\|_{H^{1}(\mathbb{T}_{h})}^{4}.

All these prove the case
\alpha=\frac{1}{2}
.

By collecting all, we conclude that

\|P_{M\leq\cdot\leq\frac{\pi}{h}}J_{41}(t^{\prime})\|_{L^{2}(\mathbb{T})}\lesssim h^{\alpha}(1+|t^{\prime}|)^{\alpha}\|\mathcal{V}_{h}^{\pm}(t^{\prime})\|_{H^{2\alpha}(\mathbb{T}_{h})}^{4},

for any
0\leq\alpha\leq\frac{1}{2}
. For
J_{42}
and
J_{43}
, similarly as (
6.17
) and (
6.18
), respectively, but using (
6.5
), we obtain

\displaystyle\|P_{M\leq\cdot\leq\frac{\pi}{h}}J_{42}(t^{\prime})\|_{L^{2}(\mathbb{T})}\lesssim

\displaystyle}{\displaystyle\left(\sum_{|k|\leq\frac{\pi}{h}}\left|\widehat{\mathcal{L}_{h}\mathcal{V}_{h}^{\pm}}-\widehat{\mathcal{V}_{h}^{\pm}}\right|^{2}\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}

\displaystyle}{\displaystyle\times\Bigg(\|\mathcal{L}_{h}\mathcal{V}_{h}^{\pm}(t^{\prime})\|_{L^{2}(\mathbb{T})}^{3}+\|\mathcal{L}_{h}\mathcal{V}_{h}^{\pm}(t^{\prime})\|_{L^{2}(\mathbb{T})}^{2}\|\mathcal{V}_{h}^{\pm}(t^{\prime})\|_{L^{2}(\mathbb{T}_{h})}

\displaystyle}{\displaystyle+\|\mathcal{L}_{h}\mathcal{V}_{h}^{\pm}(t^{\prime})\|_{L^{2}(\mathbb{T})}\|\mathcal{V}_{h}^{\pm}(t^{\prime})\|_{L^{2}(\mathbb{T}_{h})}^{2}+\|\mathcal{V}_{h}^{\pm}(t^{\prime})\|_{L^{2}(\mathbb{T}_{h})}^{3}\Bigg)

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle}{\displaystyle h^{\alpha}\|\mathcal{V}_{h}^{\pm}(t^{\prime})\|_{H^{\alpha}(\mathbb{T}_{h})}\left(\|\mathcal{L}_{h}\mathcal{V}_{h}^{\pm}(t^{\prime})\|_{L^{2}(\mathbb{T})}+\|\mathcal{V}_{h}^{\pm}(t^{\prime})\|_{L^{2}(\mathbb{T}_{h})}\right)^{3},

for any
0\leq\alpha\leq 2
, and

\displaystyle\|P_{M\leq\cdot\leq\frac{\pi}{h}}J_{43}(t^{\prime})\|_{L^{2}(\mathbb{T})}\lesssim

\displaystyle}{\displaystyle\|P_{>\frac{\pi}{h}}\mathcal{L}_{h}\mathcal{V}_{h}^{\pm}(t^{\prime})\|_{L^{2}(\mathbb{T})}\|\mathcal{L}_{h}\mathcal{V}_{h}^{\pm}(t^{\prime})\|_{L^{2}(\mathbb{T})}^{3}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle}{\displaystyle h^{\alpha}\|\mathcal{L}_{h}\mathcal{V}_{h}^{\pm}(t^{\prime})\|_{H^{\alpha}(\mathbb{T})}\|\mathcal{L}_{h}\mathcal{V}_{h}^{\pm}(t^{\prime})\|_{L^{2}(\mathbb{T})}^{2},

for any
\alpha\geq 0
. Collecting all and taking
2\alpha=s
in addition to Lemma
2.6
, we obtain

(6.29)

\displaystyle}{\displaystyle\|P_{M\leq\cdot\leq\frac{\pi}{h}}J_{4}(t^{\prime})\|_{L^{2}(\mathbb{T})}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle}{\displaystyle\int_{0}^{t}\Bigg(\|(\mathcal{L}_{h}\mathcal{V}_{h}^{\pm}-\mathcal{W}^{\pm})(t^{\prime})\|_{L^{2}(\mathbb{T})}\left(\|\mathcal{V}_{h}^{\pm}(t^{\prime})\|_{H^{s}(\mathbb{T}_{h})}+\|\mathcal{W}^{\pm}(t^{\prime})\|_{H^{s}(\mathbb{T})}\right)^{3}

\displaystyle}{\displaystyle+h^{\frac{s}{2}}(1+|t^{\prime}|)^{\frac{s}{2}}\|\mathcal{V}_{h}^{\pm}(t^{\prime})\|_{H^{s}(\mathbb{T}_{h})}^{4}+h^{\frac{s}{2}}\|\mathcal{V}_{h}^{\pm}(t^{\prime})\|_{H^{s}(\mathbb{T}_{h})}^{4}\Bigg)\;dt^{\prime}.

Collecting (
6.20
), (
6.21
), (
6.22
), and (
6.29
), one concludes (
6.19
).
∎

Proof of Proposition
3.9
.

For given
R>0
, let
u_{h,0}^{\pm}\in H^{s}(\mathbb{T}_{h})
denote the initial data satisfying

\sup_{h\in(0,1]}\big\|\big(u_{h,0}^{+},u_{h,0}^{-}\big)\big\|_{\mathbb{H}^{s}(\mathbb{T}_{h})}\leq R,

and let
T>0
be a common lifespan of solutions to (
3.5
) and (
3.6
)
11
11

11

One can take the minimum
T
appearing in Corollaries
4.12
and
6.4
. Then, by Corollaries
4.12
and
6.4
in addition to Lemma
2.6
, the solutions to (
3.5
) and (
3.6
) are uniformly (in
h
) bounded in terms of the initial data on their lifespan, i.e.,

\sup_{t\in[-T,T]}\|v_{h}^{\pm}(t)\|_{H^{s}(\mathbb{T}_{h})},\;\sup_{t\in[-T,T]}\|w^{\pm}(t)\|_{H^{s}(\mathbb{T})}\lesssim R,

as well as

(6.30)

\sup_{t\in[-T,T]}\|\mathcal{V}^{\pm}_{h}(t)\|_{H^{s}(\mathbb{T}_{h})},\;\sup_{t\in[-T,T]}\|\mathcal{W}^{\pm}(t)\|_{H^{s}(\mathbb{T})}\lesssim R,

where
\mathcal{V}^{\pm}_{h}(t)
and
\mathcal{W}^{\pm}(t)
are defined as in (
5.1
) and (
5.10
), respectively. Note that

\mathcal{L}_{h}v_{h}^{\pm}(t)-w^{\pm}(t)=\left(\mathcal{L}_{h}S_{h}^{\pm}(t)\mathcal{V}^{\pm}_{h}(t)-S^{\pm}(t)\mathcal{L}_{h}\mathcal{V}^{\pm}_{h}(t)\right)+\left(S^{\pm}(t)\mathcal{L}_{h}\mathcal{V}^{\pm}_{h}(t)-S^{\pm}(t)\mathcal{W}^{\pm}(t)\right).

By Proposition
6.11
and (
6.30
), we know

\|\left(\mathcal{L}_{h}S_{h}^{\pm}(t)\mathcal{V}^{\pm}_{h}-S^{\pm}(t)\mathcal{L}_{h}\mathcal{V}^{\pm}_{h}\right)(t)\|_{L^{2}(\mathbb{T})}\lesssim_{R}h^{\frac{2s}{5}}.

Thus, it suffices to show that there exists
0<h_{0}=h_{0}(R)<1
sufficiently small such that

(6.31)

\|\mathcal{L}_{h}\mathcal{V}^{\pm}_{h}(t)-\mathcal{W}^{\pm}(t)\|_{C_{t}([-T,T];L^{2}(\mathbb{T}))}\lesssim_{R}h^{\frac{2s}{5}}

for all
0<h\leq h_{0}
. Here, time
T>0
could be smaller than the one appearing in (
6.30
). Fix
M>0
, sufficiently large and depending only on
R
, such that

\frac{C}{M^{\frac{1}{2}}}\left(1+R\right)^{2}\leq\frac{1}{4},

where
C>0
is a universal constant appeared in Propositions
6.10
,
6.12
, and
6.13
, and (
6.30
), which is independent not only on
h
, but also on
R
. For this
M
, take
h_{0}
satisfying
h_{0}M<\pi
. For
h\leq h_{0}
, by Propositions
6.10
,
6.12
, and
6.13
in addition to (
6.30
), we obtain

\displaystyle}{\displaystyle\|\mathcal{L}_{h}\mathcal{V}^{\pm}_{h}(t)-\mathcal{W}^{\pm}(t)\|_{L^{2}(\mathbb{T})}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle}{\displaystyle\|P_{\leq M}(\mathcal{L}_{h}\mathcal{V}^{\pm}_{h}-\mathcal{W}^{\pm})(t)\|_{L^{2}(\mathbb{T})}+\|P_{M\leq\cdot\leq\frac{\pi}{h}}(\mathcal{L}_{h}\mathcal{V}^{\pm}_{h}-\mathcal{W}^{\pm})(t)\|_{L^{2}(\mathbb{T})}+\|P_{\geq\frac{\pi}{h}}(\mathcal{L}_{h}\mathcal{V}^{\pm}_{h}-\mathcal{W}^{\pm})(t)\|_{L^{2}(\mathbb{T})}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle}{\displaystyle Ch^{\frac{s}{2}}(1+T)^{1+\frac{s}{2}}M^{\frac{3}{2}+\frac{s}{2}}\left(1+R\right)R^{2}

\displaystyle}{\displaystyle+\frac{C}{M^{\frac{1}{2}}}\|(\mathcal{L}_{h}\mathcal{V}_{h}^{\pm}-\mathcal{W}^{\pm})(t^{\prime})\|_{C_{t}([-T,T];L^{2}(\mathbb{T}))}\left(1+R\right)^{2}

\displaystyle}{\displaystyle+CT\|(\mathcal{L}_{h}\mathcal{V}_{h}^{\pm}-\mathcal{W}^{\pm})(t^{\prime})\|_{C_{t}([-T,T];L^{2}(\mathbb{T}))}\left(M^{\frac{3}{2}}R+\left(1+R\right)^{3}\right),

for any
0\leq t\leq T
.

For fixed
M>0
, we can take
T>0
sufficiently small such that

CT\left(M^{\frac{3}{2}}R+\left(1+R\right)^{3}\right)\leq\frac{1}{4}.

Consequently, we obtain

\|\mathcal{L}_{h}\mathcal{V}^{\pm}_{h}(t)-\mathcal{W}^{\pm}(t)\|_{L^{2}(\mathbb{T})}\leq 2Ch^{\frac{s}{2}}(1+T)^{1+\frac{s}{2}}M^{\frac{3}{2}+\frac{s}{2}}\left(1+R\right)R^{2},

which guarantees (
6.31
).
∎
